NEW: `ladder` (a firm's tranches by kind — bond, sub bond, loan, paper, facility — with the maturity wall by year and the blended rate; a sovereign's stock by tenor, of GDP), `curves` (the shape today against a month and a year ago, tenor table with the moves in bp and the swap spread, what it hangs off), `lines` (a firm's product lines: share, edge, capacity vs sold vs expected vs stock, each a market link), `sellers` (a market's named sellers by share, the pool, the sellers abroad), `macro` (the region's dashboard in eight sections), `markets`/`pools`/`labour`/`banks`/`firms`/`funds`/`books` (the region's lists — each four lines over the screener, region column hidden), `links` (what it is tied to: bank and lines, parent, subsidiaries, funds run, estate, offerings; a fund's manager, bank, index, portfolio; a region's institutions, banks, pairs, indices, lanes), `contracts` (the book by party, by reference — a commodity's futures, a pair's forwards, a firm's CDS — or by region, with the mark settled), `diag` (the damper's bound names by book and streak, the dead ceilings). **The shell** (`Aurora.tsx`): the grammar is `<object> [function] [arg]` — the LONGEST leading run that names an object exactly wins (so `usa retail pool` is the pool, not `usa pools retail`), words after the object that are not a function fall to search — then a BARE function (or unique prefix: `stat`, `mac`) on the open object with its argument under the function's own `argKey` (`chart 10y` → series, `statements treasury` → tab, `all productLines` → path), then a KIND's word (`estates`, `books`, `central banks`, `pairs`) to the screener over all of them (an empty kind says so); tapping the bar with an object open lists its functions with a line each (the menu the user asked for — the input is blurred after a submit so the next tap fires it); the home has what happened, the regions with unemployment, the curves with 10y, the central banks with the rate, commodities and pairs with their prints, the banks, the largest firms and funds, every kind with its count, recents; the trail collapses repeats; a home button beside back. **Resolver order** (`refOfIdentifier`): `type:id` → every module's parse → region → company → fund → the id kinds; a commodity's single name-word (`oil`) resolves only when it names one. **Verified** by a Chromium tour over every function on a region, a firm, a bank, a fund, a market, a pool, an occupation, a commodity, a pair, a curve, a central bank, a lane, a tranche, a contract, an index, a book (`tour3.mjs`, 100+ screenshots at 390×844, zero page errors), and the screenshots READ: the fixes that reading found were the menu not opening on a refocused input, headers truncating in six-column tables (column weights on the module's columns; region columns hidden in region lists; three narrow tables in `lines` instead of two wide ones), a KV whose value was a long link, raw instrument ids where a ticker exists (`instrumentName`, `partyName`), due dates as "Apr 27". Gates: tsc clean, lint at the ratchet (354), hygiene clean, 123 tests, the bundle builds. **Kept out of the repo's source:** the tour script lives under `node_modules/.cache/aurora-smoke/` (playwright-core resolves from there). **The kinks WIP** (labour level target in units — `lastWeekUtilisation`, stage08 write-back, outputNeedHeads = cap × utilisation × demand pull) is saved as `docs/wip/labour-level-target-in-units.patch` so it survives the container; it is unmeasured and not applied.
349. **CLOSE C1 — THE AUDIT IS BUILT AND THE BASELINE READ (2026-09-02, user: "Why are there still unbacked or boundaries… I want a truly closed model even if stuff will blow up for a while. Confirm and go").** `scripts/audit/{types,money,ownership,prices,accounts,names,index}.ts` — the six families of §5-CLOSE, run by the harness every week (`auditWeek`, each finding a violation) with the scoreboard at the end (`auditSummary`). **The baseline, 13 weeks, shocks off, this tree — "the money that is not anyone's" 2,100B across 31 lines in the last week:** M1 the central bank fails to close in every region every week (JPN reserves 141B + TGA 28B against 41B of assets; worst 308B) — the seed adds every deposit class to reserves 1:1 while the central bank holds 15% of the stock; M2 wholesale owed to nobody 574B at worst (USA 560B); the boundary roll ("wholesale funding raised/repaid") every week, −115B at worst; household income from nobody 14.8% of income; tax from nobody up to 25.8B/wk from week 9; coupon to nobody 0.2B/wk; M4 26 firms overdrawn (−2.3B; ZBJV −397M), one fund −4.8B in one week, JPN pools −0.7B; M6 JPN's money stock moved 8B in a week with no lending and no central bank purchase behind it. **Clean already:** M3 (the trial balance — firms', funds', pools' and households' cash ARE the banks' lines), M5 (every bank's sheet closes), O1 for bonds, loans and paper (within 2%), F3, X2. **Ownership:** O1 sovereign under-held in JPN (−9.6%, paper with no owner — its coupon is the M2 line); O2 193 firms with MORE shares on the register than they issued (18B of phantom stock — a real defect, the register minting equity); O3/O4 rows and loans on merged or dead firms (small, 0.01B); O5 606 derivative contracts with a dead party (0.4B), 819 consignments in transit to or from a firm that is gone. **Prices:** P1 1,052 of 2,459 issuers price a senior claim wider than a junior one — the bank FACILITY margin (350bp) above the same firm's bond OAS on most; P2 468 names with a CDS 150bp+ from the bond; P3 Spearman(OAS, rating) 0.45 in JPN; P4 15 equities and 2 CP names bound 8+ weeks. **Accounts:** F2 the treasury's account moves by nothing like revenue − outlays + borrowing + remittance (JPN: 0.00B moved against 7.4B expected — the TGA is pinned); F1 54 firms file a cash line that is not their balance (small). **Names:** 496 firms carry a `parent-hash` id and 792 tranches are named for another firm (the padding clones). The order of closure is §5-CLOSE's; C2 (the seed) starts now.
350. **CLOSE C2 — THE SEED CLOSES (2026-09-02).** `src/engine/bootstrap/close-seed.ts`, run after every book is seeded: (1) each bank's household deposit line is what its asset side needs after equity, secured funding and the real corporate/institutional/segment balances — wholesale opens at ZERO (the seed's "unnamed lender" was the household sector all along; USA deposits 664B → 1,168B, household net worth up by the same); (2) the central bank's sovereign book is set to reserves + treasury account − FX reserves (USA 100.5B → 346.1B: QE at birth, the paper real issuance to the central bank), currency is a STORED liability at zero, and `unbackedBankCashUSD` and the currency-residual function are DELETED — `centralBankIdentityResidualUSD` is the one function and the audit's M1 asserts it to the dollar; (3) the sovereign stock outstanding is what the named holders hold, bucket by bucket (USA 670B → 916B, 108% of GDP), so no coupon is owed to nobody at birth. At week 0 the identity closes in every region (reserves 305.9 + TGA 45.6 = 346.1 + 5.4 FX, USA). **Measured, 13 weeks:** "the money that is not anyone's" 2,100B → 706B; M1's residual is now only what the WEEK creates — 55–70B by w13 (the bank's second money engine and the funding close, C3/C4) against 128–308B; wholesale rebuilds to 280B at worst through the funding close's boundary raise (up to 102B in one week — C3's first target); M2 tax/income/interest from nobody unchanged (C5); 123 tests pass.
351. **CLOSE C3 — THE BOUNDARY PARTY IS GONE (2026-09-02).** `UNMODELED` left `PartyRef` (compile-loud: `PARTY_KINDS`, `PARTY_REGISTRY`, settlement's switch), and with it `unmodeledByReason`, `unmodeledDeltaByRegion`, `unmodeledDepositDeltaByBank`, the sheet's `unmodeledDepositsUSD`, the harness's `BOUNDARY_FRONTIERS`/`checkBoundaryFlows`, and `wholesaleFundingUSD` itself — a bank has no "wholesale lender" any more. **What replaced each site:** (1) the funding close — a bank short of its buffer after every real flow has settled borrows from the CENTRAL BANK, unsecured, at the window rate plus `CENTRAL_BANK_LOAN_PENALTY_BPS` (100): `centralBankLoanUSD` on the bank, `loansToBanksUSD` on the central bank's asset side (inside `centralBankAssetsUSD`, so the reserves it creates are backed by construction), drawn as CENTRAL_BANK → BANK_SECURITIES, repaid from cash above the buffer (BANK_SECURITIES → CENTRAL_BANK, in 02b), its interest a BANK → CENTRAL_BANK payment — `raiseCentralBankLoanUSD`/`repayCentralBankLoanUSD`; the evolution no longer moves cash for funding interest; a resolution never haircuts the central bank (the treasury's guarantee covers the shortfall; test updated). The interbank unsecured market (surplus banks lending first) is the recorded refinement, not a boundary. (2) The FX desks' client margin was booked as wholesale funding; it is `clientMarginUSD`, a liability to the clients that posted it. (3) `05-unit-bidding`'s "seed supplier" fallback is `defect()`: a seller that cannot be paid fails at the site that made the key (none fired in 13 weeks). (4) `book-settlement`'s leftover guard: rounding dust (−1,000 USD on 124M of fees on the first run) goes to the desks that earned the fees; a leftover past `max(10k, 1e-6 × net)` is a defect. (5) stage 08's `post` with no counterparty is a defect; its one caller — the inventory carrying cost a region with no distributor "paid to nobody" — no longer pays: the firm warehouses its own stock inside its own opex. (6) The weekly re-derivation of wholesale as the funding RESIDUAL in `evolveBankingSector` is deleted outright — no funding line is written from an identity any more. **Measured, 13 weeks (this tree):** no boundary line, no wholesale line; M1's weekly residual is the bank's second money engine (C4): USA reserves + TGA exceed the central bank's book by 45–69B at w13; the removal of the boundary EXPOSES overdrafts — 76 firms overdrawn (−18B, CLGT −6.4B) and one fund (−20B) that the boundary used to fund silently; O1 loans 2.4% over in USA (a ledger minting claims — the facility/loan sync); 123 tests pass.
352. **CLOSE C4 — THE BANK'S SECOND MONEY ENGINE IS GONE (2026-09-02).** `evolveBankingSector` moves no cash of its own any more. What it DECIDES it now REPORTS on the sheet (`reservesInterestWeeklyUSD`, `corporateDepositInterestWeeklyUSD`, `dividendWeeklyUSD`) and 02b PAYS as instructions between named parties: interest on reserves CENTRAL_BANK → BANK (the central bank's expense — it settles by creating the reserves, and its remittance to the treasury was already net of it, so the identity is unchanged and the flow is on the ledger); interest on corporate balances BANK → COMPANY, pro rata to every positive balance at that bank; the dividend to the REGISTER through the paying agent (`payHoldersCash(bank.id, 'EQUITY')` — the same path every issuer's dividend takes; reserves and equity leave at settlement, each dollar lands on a holder of record). Household loan interest is a DEBIT OF THE BORROWERS' DEPOSITS at the lending bank (deposits −, equity +; no reserve moves and nothing arrives from outside); the itemized business slice the evolution used to credit is zero by construction (every business loan is a facility or an SME pool, both paid through settlement). `bank-lending.ts` writes NO reserves: the household return is deposits ± (mortgage origination − discharge + card/term origination − amortization); the facility residue (a loan that left the ladders with no payment) is a WRITE-OFF through `bookPnL`, and a loan that APPEARED with no payment is `defect()` (none fired in 13 weeks). `creditUnbacked`, `UnbackedLedger`, `newUnbackedLedger` and `ctx.unbackedLedger` are DELETED: the PE take-private proceeds are a payment INSTITUTION (the sponsor, which the LPs' calls had already funded) → HOUSEHOLD; the three "unmapped issuer" counters in the register's paying agent are `defect()` (a holder paid by an issuer nobody can name is money from nobody). The audit moved to `src/engine/audit/` (the one-script hygiene rule; `scripts/` holds the harness and the hygiene check only) and `close-seed.ts` joined the bootstraps the ledger rule allows. **Measured, 13 weeks (this tree):** O1 loans held = outstanding is CLEAN (the 2.4% USA over-holding was the facility residue moving reserves); X2 clean; "the money that is not anyone's" 315B/23 lines (from 260B/24 — see next). **M1 DID NOT MOVE, and its shape is now legible:** the per-region residuals SUM to near zero most weeks (w13: USA −60.5, EUR −21.3, UK +32.4, JPN +59.8 = +10.4B) — what remains is not creation but CROSS-BORDER SETTLEMENT WITHOUT A CENTRAL-BANK LEG: an importer's bank in one region pays an exporter's bank in another, reserves leave one central bank's system and appear in another's, and neither book records the claim. The world-level residual (−8 to −22B, spiking +85B/+136B in weeks 6 and 12) is the treasury's quarterly credit from nobody (M2 tax, C5) plus small change. Also exposed: one fund at −52B (M4) — the audit's next read names it. Next: C4b, the official-settlement leg (the receiving central bank's foreign claim = the paying central bank's foreign-official deposit, one signed line per book, summing to zero across the world), then C5.
353. **CLOSE C4b — OFFICIAL SETTLEMENT: THE CROSS-BORDER LEG (2026-09-02).** A payer in one region paying a payee in another moved reserves out of one central bank's system and into another's, and neither book recorded it — that was the whole of M1's remaining shape (§7.352: USA −60B against JPN +60B, summing to zero). Now `settlement.ts` reads the two sides of every instruction for the central-bank system each lives in (`regionOfParty`: a firm's or fund's region, a bank's, the region a pool/household/treasury/central bank IS; the clearing house has none, so a leg through it attributes by its other side) and books the net per region on the central bank as `foreignOfficialClaimsUSD` — one signed line per book (positive: claims on other central banks; negative: their deposits here), inside `centralBankAssetsUSD`, summing to zero across the world by construction. The audit's M1 adds the households' money IN TRANSIT to their banks (settled this week, on a bank's book next — `pendingBankSettlementUSD`) to the identity's liability side, and asserts the world's claims net to zero; M4 names the worst overdrawn fund. **Measured, 13 weeks (this tree):** week 1 closes in every region to −0.5B and better (from −56/+8/+7/+34B); the worst residual is 39B (from 69B), and its shape is now WORLD-LEVEL and periodic — −10 to −25B in ordinary weeks (USA −11, EUR −5, UK −3, JPN −3 at w2), +39/+13/+14/+14B in weeks 6 and 12 — which is the treasury's statement-driven account (revenue by formula at the quarter, outlays by accrual every week) rather than money moving: C5's target. Exposed by name: a hedge fund (FOBG) overdrawn by 52B — a fund paying money it does not have, which no payment gate refuses (the M4 slice after C5). "The money that is not anyone's" 315B → 172B.
354. **CLOSE C5 — THE TREASURY AND THE HOUSEHOLDS ARE PAYMENTS (2026-09-02).** (1) **The treasury's account moves by payments and nothing else.** The statement `central-bank.ts` posted (revenue by formula, outlays by accrual, the settled legs subtracted back out) is deleted; settlement's `treasuryFlowsByRegion` (per region, by reason — the same ledger households have) is the treasury's flow statement, carried on `state.lastSettlement`. `governmentRevenueUSD` is the collections that arrived (`unmodeledTaxRevenueUSD` — a quarter of the take, "the bases the model cannot tax" — is DELETED); `governmentInterestToUnmodeledHoldersUSD` is deleted (every tranche is held, a coupon reaches a holder of record or is not paid). (2) **The central bank is a holder like any other.** Its matured paper is PAID — GOVERNMENT → CENTRAL_BANK at redemption (the PUB2b comment said "a CB asset and a CB liability fall together" while the code wrote only the asset: every week the central bank's paper vanished from its book, the treasury kept the money, and the reinvestment bought new paper with reserves created against nothing — the creator M1 showed once the statement stopped masking it: +57B USA by w13); the treasury pays it its coupon weekly on the accrual (the one holder whose date does not matter); the remittance is a PAYMENT (CENTRAL_BANK → GOVERNMENT, or the treasury covering a loss) of coupons + bill accretion − the interest on reserves actually paid − the reverse-repo interest paid, so the central bank keeps no retained earnings and its assets are exactly its liabilities; the standing facility's lending is its asset (`standingFacilityLentUSD`, derived from the repo book by the session, one writer). (3) **Households.** The residual "return path" income (`unmodeledCapitalReceiptShareOfIncome`/`…ResidualAnnualUSD`, 14.7% of income from nobody) is DELETED — income is what arrived; the placeholder wealth (`unmodeledFinancialAssetsUSD`, assets nobody issued) is DELETED — wealth is the claims that exist; the yield-times-mark dividend estimate is replaced by the dividends the public float was PAID: the register's paying agent now pays the float's share of every dividend to the issuer's region's households (`dividend to the public float`) and the register's holders their share of the ISSUED stock (they used to receive the whole dividend on a part of it), read back off the household flow ledger (`lastWeekHouseholdDividendsUSD`). (4) **The audit's memory.** The harness handed the audit the same state object it mutates in place, so "previous week" was this week (F2 read the treasury moving by zero): the audit keeps its own snapshot (`audit/snapshot.ts`); F2 asserts the account moved by exactly its payments and that revenue is the tax remitted; M4 flags an overdrawn treasury. **Measured, 13 weeks (this tree, with C6):** M2 CLEAN — no line "from nobody" or "to nobody" remains; F2 "the account moves by its payments" clean every week; M1 worst 20.6B (one week, USA w12; ≤ 9B otherwise; ≤ 0.25B in week 1) from 308B at the baseline and 39B after C4b; "the money that is not anyone's" 152B/13 lines (from 2,100B/31). **What remains, by name:** M1's residual is small and PERIODIC — negative in weeks 2–3 (−5B USA), a one-week +20B at the quarter that reverses — a timing effect between two legs of one flow, not a creator (C5b); F2 revenue ≠ tax remitted in 3 quarter weeks (JPN 4.1B vs 2.9B — a payer that could not be found, or a tax reported by a firm that died); M4: a hedge fund overdrawn (25B at worst, 3B in the last week) and the JPN treasury −3B in one week — payments no gate refuses, the M4 slice; M6 does not yet count the money banks create by paying interest into deposits and by paying out of their own account; O2's phantom shares are unchanged (the register minting equity).
355. **CLOSE C6 — A CLONE IS A FIRM WITH ITS OWN NAME (2026-09-02).** `companyGenerator.ts`'s padding clones carried the parent's id plus a hash (496 firms named for another firm) and the parent's tranche ids (792 tranches named for another firm), with the parent's UNSCALED principals. A clone's id is `REGION_TICKER`, its tranches carry ITS ticker (`FNFT-T1` → `EBGU-T1`), and its ladder is scaled with the rest of its sheet. The N2 id rule admits the carrier form (`USA_CAR_OSVB`, the only ids left). Measured: N2 tranche ids clean; N2 ids 496 → 0.
356. **CLOSE C5b — THE CENTRAL BANK CLOSES (2026-09-02).** A per-stage trace of the identity (`central-bank-identity-trace.ts`, `CB_IDENTITY_TRACE=1`: after every stage, each region's reserves + treasury account + currency + money in transit − assets, with the component that moved) named the last writer in one run: `02-region-macro`, ±5–8B a week with a four-week period, entirely in the IN-TRANSIT term. The household rebuild in `evolution.ts` takes a fixed field list and never named `pendingBankSettlementUSD`, so the households' money settled after the bank pass was dropped every week before 02b could post the banks' T+1 leg — the payer's reserves had left and no bank received them: money destroyed when households were net receivers, created when they were net payers. One field carried. **Measured, 13 weeks (this tree):** M1's worst residual is 0.88B (from 308B at the baseline, 39B after C4b, 20.6B after C5) and what remains is the one-week lag between the central-bank stage's payments (its coupon, its remittance) and the settlement that executes them next week, plus the bill accretion booked a stage before its remittance; "the money that is not anyone's" 143B/13 lines (from 2,100B/31) — and every remaining line is an OVERDRAFT (M4: 92 firms −10B, two funds −8B, JPN pools −0.9B, the JPN treasury −3B in one week), an M6 decomposition that does not yet count bank-created deposits, or O2's phantom stock; no line is a flow from or to nobody. The campaign's next slices are those three, then the price families are read as the closed world's prices.
357. **CLOSE M4 — NO BALANCE STANDS NEGATIVE AT THE CLOSE (2026-09-02).** `stages/overdraft-sweep.ts`, run right before the settlement close: every holder whose settled balance plus the net of every instruction recorded since the mid-week pass (read off the journal itself — the register's paying agent journals without touching the running net, and a quarterly dividend paid that way left JTLN −7.8B and UYIR −8.8B after a first sweep that read only `pendingNetById`) stands below zero has spent its bank's money, and that credit is named and priced in the same statement it is found: a firm's overdraft is a revolver draw at its house bank (the 02b conversion's rule, at the close), a fund's — of EVERY kind, not hedge funds only — is a prime-brokerage draw at its broker, past the struck line at `OVERDRAFT_PENALTY_BPS` (200) over the standing terms (the old close sweep capped the draw at the line and left the rest overdrawn: FOBG −25B), a pool's is an SME facility draw at the region's banks by their share. Each is a loan that creates a deposit (BANK_CREDIT → the holder, the asset on the bank's live sheet). The treasury cannot overdraw either: settlement's own apply carries a WAYS AND MEANS advance on the central bank (`waysAndMeansUSD`, an asset, at the policy rate, repaid by the next money in) — F2 reads the account net of it. **Measured, 13 weeks:** M4 CLEAN in every kind (firms 92 → 0 overdrawn, funds 2 → 0, pools 7 → 0, the treasury 1 → 0); M1 unchanged at 0.8B; M3 clean; "the money that is not anyone's" 156B/9 lines, every line now M6's decomposition or O2's phantom stock. The old hedge-fund-only close sweep (`runPrimeBrokerageCloseSweep`) is unwired.
358. **CLOSE O2 — THE REGISTER NEVER HOLDS MORE THAN THE ISSUE (2026-09-02).** Three writers, each fixed at its site: (1) a padding clone scaled its cap but copied the parent's share count and price, so its cap was not price × shares (up to 7×) and the seed wrote shares against the inflated cap — a clone's SHARES scale with the firm now (`companyGenerator.ts`); (2) the seed allocated every entity's equity budget over the caps and the budgets together reach 2.6× the stock — a normalization pass after the region's entities are built scales each over-held name to the issue and leaves the unplaced budget as the entity's CASH (measured at week 0 by a register audit: 242 firms over-held, 94B of stock nobody issued → 0); (3) a buyback reduced shares outstanding but never retired the register's shares — stage 08 now settles the corporate action on the holders (`settleCorporateActionOnHolders`, the same ratio the float sees through the paying agent), and the register's scaling scales the SHARE COUNT with the notional, which it never did. **Measured, 13 weeks:** O2 "shares held ≤ issued" CLEAN every week (from 186–221 firms, 20–54B); market cap = price × shares off on one firm by 0.03B.
359. **CLOSE M6 — THE MONEY STOCK MOVES ONLY BY ITS CREATORS (2026-09-02).** M6 explained Δ(deposits + treasury account) by net lending and central-bank assets alone and printed 100B gaps. Every creator is counted now, from the ledger itself: settlement reports per region the deposits bank credit wrote (`creditCreatedByRegion`), the reserves the central bank issued (`centralBankIssuanceByRegion`), what the banks paid out of or took into their OWN account — the equity legs AND the securities-account legs, since a desk buying paper from a fund destroys the fund's deposit with no P&L (`bankOwnAccountByRegion`), and money that arrived from other regions (`crossBorderByRegion`); 02b reports the deposits the household books wrote (`householdBookDepositFlowWeeklyUSD`: origination less discharge, amortization and interest) and the interest credited to deposits; the ways-and-means advance is the seventh term. **Measured, 13 weeks:** M6 from 12/13 weeks failing (worst 108B) to 5/13 (worst 12B); the remaining gaps are the one-week lags between a stage's own write and the payment that carries it (the same shape as M1's 1B). **The scoreboard's bottom line: "the money that is not anyone's" is 3.3B across 4 lines — from 2,100B across 31 at the baseline of §7.349.** Money (M) fails only M1 (1B, the lag) and M6 (the lag); every flow from or to nobody is gone.
360. **CLOSE O1 + F2 — UNPLACED PAPER IS WITHDRAWN; THE PAYROLL TAX IS PAID (2026-09-02).** (1) After each sovereign auction (07c bonds, 07f bills) the offering the market did not take is WITHDRAWN from the youngest tranches of the bucket (`withdrawUnplacedIssuance`, domain/government.ts) and the treasury's need rolls forward (`pendingUnfundedDeficitUSD`) — paper nobody bought is not debt. O1's sovereign line reads "paper with no owner" only in the two issuance weeks, when the quarter's new issue sits on the ladder for the one week before 07c places it; the audit now treats paper issued THIS week as in the auction (offered, not yet anyone's, not owed to nobody either). (2) The employer payroll tax was accrued from the macro wage bill and credited to the treasury at month end with money no employer had paid — the last "revenue from nobody" (F2: JPN 4.2B reported against 3.0B remitted in every month-end week). Every employer remits it on its own wage bill as a payment: firms in stage 08 (`employer payroll tax`, alongside the wages), SME pools in 03; stage 11 reports payroll revenue as what arrived (`ctx.payrollTaxByRegion`), and the accrual (`accruedPayrollTaxUSD`) is deleted. **Measured, 13 weeks:** F2 CLEAN in both checks (the account moves by its payments; revenue = tax remitted); the accounts family fails only F1 (a filed statement's cash line, a reporting drift). "The money that is not anyone's" 3.5B/4 lines — M1's and M6's one-week lags. Remainders by name: one tranche on a merger's acquirer still carries the target's ticker (`USA_LUUI-OPPORTUNISTIC-8` on OTYC — 10-mergers moves ladders without re-keying); P1's flat 350bp facility margin is the next line.
361. **CLOSE P1/P2/N2 — THE FACILITY IS PRICED; THE BASIS READS PRICES; A MERGER RE-KEYS (2026-09-02).** (1) `REVOLVER_MARGIN_BPS` (350, one stated margin for every borrower — the last stated price on a loan) is replaced at every site (ten in stage 08, the 02b overdraft conversion, the close sweep) by ONE quote, `facilityMarginBpsFor`: the borrower's own default probability (the hazard the credit market prices), the lending bank's cost of equity, the region's realised recoveries, through `quoteLoanMarginBps` — the same price the SME book gets; carried as a lane (`facilityMarginBps`) into the back kernel. (2) The CDS basis test reads only names whose protection book CLEARED this week (`cdsClearedWeek`): a name with no hedging demand carries last print, a quote, and 07h's cleared spread is the one writer (stage 08 carries it forward). (3) A merger re-keys the target's protected tranches to the acquirer (`ACQ<week>-<old id>`, the position re-pointed) — the last N2 line. **Measured, 13 weeks:** O1 CLEAN (the auction-week read); P1 990 → 945 inversions — and the remaining ones are now legible: a facility priced off the PD moves the week the PD moves, while the bond's OAS is DAMPED to a 25% weekly move and lags it (JPGN: facility 6,134bp against a bond still at 1,250bp; PYIM's loan and bond, two damped books, 1,386 against 1,250). The dampers on every clearing book (bonds 25%, sovereigns 20%, loans 25%, equity 18%, swaps 25%) are clamps on prices (rule 2) and P4 already names them (names bound 8+ weeks); they are the next slice, and P1/P2/P3 are re-read after it.
362. **CLOSE — THE THIRD CYCLE, AND WHAT THE LAST BILLION IS (2026-09-02).** The funding close, the resolution and the central bank's own week post payments after the close and settled NEXT week — the one-week lag M1 and M6 printed. A third settlement cycle (`settlement-funding`, after the central-bank stage) settles a week's money inside the week: week 1 closes to −0.14B USA (from −0.5B). **What the run then shows is not a lag but a slow, monotonic drift** — USA −0.14 → −1.86B over 13 weeks, assets EXCEEDING money by ~0.14B a week: the central bank's income that the remittance does not carry, kept as retained earnings its identity has no line for — the interest on the lender-of-last-resort loans (02b's `central bank loan interest`) and the standing facility's repo interest. The remittance is coupons + accretion + ways-and-means interest − interest on reserves − reverse-repo interest; those two are added next, and the central bank keeps nothing. Scoreboard: money fails only M1 (this drift) and M6 (5/13 weeks, ≤13B — the same lag family, re-read after); "the money that is not anyone's" 5.3B/5 lines.
363. **CLOSE — THE CENTRAL BANK CLOSES TO THE DOLLAR, EVERY WEEK (2026-09-02).** The drift of §7.362 was the central bank's own income kept as retained earnings: the interest on its lender-of-last-resort loans (`lastLoanInterestUSD`, from 02b's payment) and on the standing facility's repo contracts (`lastStandingFacilityInterestUSD`, from the session's maturities) join the remittance, and the central bank keeps nothing. **Measured, 13 weeks (this tree): M1 CLEAN in all 13 weeks and all four regions — assets = reserves + treasury account + money in transit, to within the audit's 1e-4 tolerance, from a 308B residual at the baseline.** The money family fails only M6 (5 of 13 weeks, ≤13B, always POSITIVE: a creator the decomposition does not yet count — the households' money in transit, credited to the household sector at settlement and to a bank's line the week after, so the bank lines rise with no leg the week the T+1 posts). "The money that is not anyone's": 1.66B on ONE line (M6's), from 2,100B across 31 at §7.349. **The campaign's scoreboard, baseline → now:** M1 308B → 0; M2 (income, tax, interest, currency, wholesale, boundary — six lines from nobody) → none exist; M3 → clean; M4 (76 firms, a fund at −52B, pools, the treasury) → clean; M5 clean throughout; M6 100B+ → ≤13B/5 weeks; O1 → clean (the auction week read); O2 18–54B of phantom stock → clean; O3/O4/O5 the dead-party rows (0.0–0.4B) remain as the estate family's; F2 → clean; N1/N2 496 ids, 792 tranches → one merged tranche; the P family is the PRICES campaign's (§5-CLOSE (6)).
364. **CLOSE — THE MONEY FAMILY CLOSES (2026-09-02).** M6's last creator was the households' money IN TRANSIT: settled to the household sector this week, on a bank's line next week, so the bank lines rose with no leg the week the T+1 posted. Money is the bank lines, the treasury's account and the in-transit balance; counted, M6 is CLEAN in all 13 weeks. **The money family now prints one line in one week: M3, 1.66B in week 13 — firms' cash by house bank against the banks' corporate deposit lines, a one-week float the 02b reconcile reads the week after.** M1, M2, M4, M5, M6: no line. "The money that is not anyone's": 1.66B on that one line, from 2,100B across 31 at the baseline (§7.349). The closed-model campaign's money work is done; O3/O4/O5 (rows, loans, contracts and consignments on dead parties, 0.0–0.4B) are the estate family's, F1 is a filed statement's cash line (reporting), N2 is one merged tranche, and the P/X families are the PRICES campaign's (§5-CLOSE (6), task 23).
365. **NO CAPS (user order, 2026-09-02: "remove any caps, that's not the right way to fix the issue").** Every weekly move cap is gone — the engine clears at the solved level (`clearedStat = solvedStat`), and the option, its constants and the adaptive widening (§7.338/§7.342, overridden by the order and recorded here as such — rule §1.20 yields to a user order on the mechanism itself) are deleted from every book: bonds, sovereigns, loans, equity, bills, swaps, CDS, futures, FX, stock loan, repo, ETF baskets, FX hedging. Everything that READ the cap as a number now reads a MEASUREMENT: the desks' one-week price risk in an underwriting fee is this week's realised move of the name; the stock-loan gap is the name's realised weekly volatility (the book's median for a name without history); the authorised participants' capacity is the equity book's median realised move; the prime brokers' haircuts are twice the region's median realised weekly price move (equity), twice the median realised weekly spread move over a five-year duration plus the repo desk's five-year sovereign haircut (credit), the repo desk's blended protection (sovereigns) — off the price ring (52 weeks) and the OAS ring (8 weeks); the index fund commits at its reference price and an overspend is a margin draw the close sweep names. **Measured, 13 weeks, no caps: it runs.** Damper binds 0 (from 1,346). P1 990 → 700 inversions (the facility lines are gone from the examples; what remains is loan against bond, two books clearing separately). P3's rank against ratings fell to 0.31 (the market disagrees with the rating more, which is the market's to say). P2 reads one name: with free prices the protection book's hedging demand nearly vanished (one name cleared) — the CDS book is the PRICES campaign's. **Exposed: M5.** Eleven bank sheets in eleven weeks no longer close (worst −89.6B, EUR:PKEH −2.4B at w13) — a mark on an asset with no equity leg, small under the caps and now full-sized; the bank-identity trace names the stage next. Stated numbers the closure introduced and still owes a derivation: `OVERDRAFT_PENALTY_BPS` 200 (the close sweep's penalty over the struck line), `CENTRAL_BANK_LOAN_PENALTY_BPS` 100 (the window's penalty over the standing facility), the sweep's 350bp SME fallback, `CREDIT_COLLATERAL_DURATION_YEARS` 5 and the 2σ convention in the haircuts, `YIELD_LIKE_MIN_WEEKLY_MOVE_BPS` 25 (pre-existing, the smallest repricing a lender assumes).
366. **M5 CLOSED UNDER FREE PRICES — A LINE IS A LINE (2026-09-02).** The bank-identity trace (`BANK_IDENTITY_TRACE_BANK=XVXT`) named the leg: at the prime-brokerage stage the broker's loan asset fell by exactly the close sweep's draws of the week before. The morning pass rebuilt the book from the HEDGE FUNDS alone, while the M4 sweep lends to a fund of any kind — so every other fund's line vanished the next morning: the broker's asset fell by the draw (M5, eleven sheets), and the fund kept the money for nothing. Every fund with a line is re-struck now, repaid from cash above its sleeve and priced, whoever the borrower is. **Measured, 13 weeks, no caps: M1–M5 no line; M6 one week (EUR, 2.9B — a one-week lag); "the money that is not anyone's" 2.93B on one line.** Free prices stand: P1 798 inversions (loan against bond, two books), P3's rank against ratings 0.37–0.48, the CDS book cleared three names. Those are the PRICES campaign's (§5-CLOSE (6)), now with nothing between the books and the market.
367. **WIRES W1 — THE WIRE LEDGER, MONEY FIRST (2026-09-02).** `src/engine/ledger/wire.ts`: a columnar `WireJournal` (wire number = journal base + row; from, to, asset kind, asset, quantity, price, reason, settle week), `wirePush` (a wire moves a positive quantity at a finite price between two different parties, or it is a defect at the caller) and `wire()` for a stage with a `PartyRef` in hand; core.ts installs the week's journal as the ACTIVE one before the first stage and persists `state.nextWireId` and `state.lastWires` (count and value by kind, and the money tail recorded after the last pass) at the end. **Every money row is a wire:** `journalPush` — the one writer of the payment journal, under `pay`, `payByIds` and the paying agent's `journalPayment` — writes the wire first, so a payment row without a wire number cannot exist. A party "paying itself" (the SME tier buying its own output) is no wire and no row: nothing moved between parties. The audit's W family: W1 asserts the week's money wires equal settlement's gross, net of the after-cutoff tail (the player's trades and the tenders recorded after the third pass settle next week — dated wires, principle N's first instance). **Measured, 13 weeks: W1 no line; the money family unchanged (M6 once, 2.9B).** The counter runs at about 300,000 wires a week.
368. **WIRES W2 — THE SECURITIES LEDGER: THE REGISTER SEALED, EVERY HOLDING MOVES BY WIRE (2026-09-02).** `src/engine/ledger/holdings-ledger.ts` is the one place a register row moves. The store is sealed: `V2World.holdings` is a `ReadonlyHoldingStore` (every column read-only — a stage writing a holding column does not compile), `mutableHoldings()` the ledger's own handle, and hygiene rule 5 fails any file outside `engine/ledger/`, `engine2/`, `holdings-store.ts` or `core.ts` that imports a row mutator. Five operations, each a numbered wire between two `PartyRef`s: `transferHolding` (from one holder to another; both books written when both are register holders), `issueHolding` (issuer → holder: the paper is CREATED, the issuer's book never debited), `retireHolding` (holder → issuer: DESTROYED, the issuer's book never credited), `clearedBookDelta` (a clearing book's fills: the holder's position after against before, per instrument, wired against the region's CLEARING_HOUSE — bought or sold, one number each), and `markHolding` (a change of VALUE with no change of quantity — accretion, a NAV mark — no wire, retired by P when value is price × quantity by construction); `closeEmptyPositions` drops a written-down book's sub-$1 residues without a wire. The unit is the registry's fact `heldInShares(type)` (`domain/assets`): shares at a price for equity and fund shares, face at 1 for paper. **The writers routed:** the holdings store's write-back (what the auctions claimed off a book against what they appended, grouped by type and region, one wire per instrument against the house); the dealer desks' fills (`applyDealerDeskFills`: the desk's position after the session against before, per cleared name, BANK_SECURITIES ↔ CLEARING_HOUSE, units at the cleared price for the equity book, face for the par books — `DESK_BOOK_KIND` names each book's kind); the primary's asset leg (`PrimaryTake.asset`, built by `primaryAssetOf`: the issuer delivers the whole take to the clearing house — a bill at FACE against its discounted money, the difference being the borrowing cost the yield implies); the lead's underwriting residual (issuer → BANK_SECURITIES of the lead, beside the money leg); ETF creations and redemptions (issued by / retired to the fund), the in-kind basket (fund → investor by transfer), the NAV mark; the merger's share exchange (the target's paper retired to the target, the acquirer's issued — never a re-key in place) and the spin-off's distribution; the estate's recoveries and write-offs (retired to the company); the sovereign redemption (retired to the treasury) and the bill accretion (a mark); the paying agent's pro-rata actions (one retirement or placement per instrument per holder). **Two defects the build found in itself, by a new instrument** — `REGISTER_DUMP=<file>` in the harness writes every institution's register at `STATE_DUMP_WEEK` (entity, type, instrument, notional, shares), and a diff of two trees at week 4 names the first row that differs: (a) issue and retire routed through `transferHolding`, which credits an issuer that is itself a register holder — an ETF retiring its shares booked 4.8B of ITS OWN shares as an asset, its NAV followed, and the O family lit up four weeks later (O2 126 firms, O1 bonds/loans/paper minting, M1/M2 as banks failed downstream) — hence the create/destroy semantics above; (b) the in-kind redemption read every redeemer's basket off the fund's rows and moved them afterwards, so later redeemers' slices (renormalised against the reduced total) were cut from rows already promised — the small-cap ETFs' constituents over-delivered 2–3x by week 4; each basket now moves at the moment it is sliced, as the in-place scaling used to. A third, caught by reading: the paying agent applied its ratio once per ROW of an instrument through a ledger that scales every row of the instrument, which would have minted a placement N-fold on N rows — the actions are aggregated per instrument before the wire. **W1 re-measured at HEAD: FAILING 13/13** (week 1: money wires 2167B against settlement's gross 1987B) — §7.367's "no line" was wrong. Root cause: stage 08's in-process shards journal through `journalPush` (wire written) into a shard journal that the merge then folded through `journalPush` again, so every shard row carried two wires; `journalAppendRow` folds the ROWS (settlement.ts), the barrier-mode replay uses it too, and a worker thread now installs a scratch wire journal so its rows can come back through `journalPush` (one real wire each, on the main thread). **The W2 audit line:** `state.lastWires.houseNetUSDByKey` — what each region's clearing house holds of each kind after the week's wires (received − delivered) — must be zero; it is not yet, by design of what is wired: GOV_BOND 3–31B per region (the banks' sovereign investment books, the central bank's fills and the treasuries' bill takes are not register holders and wire nothing yet — W3's register-per-tranche work), EQUITY (the households' fills — the household register), and a 0.0–0.8B residual on CORP_BOND/LEVERAGED_LOAN/COMMERCIAL_PAPER to be named in W3. Also: the harness prints every violation under `VERBOSE=1` (the six-line cap hid the W family entirely); the twelve new reasons (desk fills, the paying agent's pro-rata actions, the central bank's loans and remittance, the treasury covering its loss) carry categories; the literal-comparison budget ratchets 56 → 54. **Measured, 13 weeks, against a fresh HEAD baseline run the same day:** week-4 registers equal to the cent in value (shares to 1e-9); the scoreboard family for family the baseline's — M6 once (2.93B), O1 sovereign once (34.05B, the UK bill book — a HEAD line), O2 market cap once (0.14B), O4/O5/P/X/F as before, "the money that is not anyone's" 2.93B on 1 line; W1 no line; W2 13/13 as the open line above; 114 violations against HEAD's 118.
369. **WIRES W3 — THE TRANCHE LEDGER: THE LADDER SEALED, EVERY PRINCIPAL MOVE A WIRE (2026-09-02).** `src/engine/ledger/tranche-ledger.ts` is the one place a ladder row's principal moves. A tranche is not an asset kind of its own: it is the issuer's record of the PAPER the register or a bank's book holds, so its wires carry that paper's kind — `trancheKindOf` in `domain/assets` (bank facility, commercial paper, floating loan, fixed bond; `BANK_FACILITY` joins the wire kinds, `TRANCHE` leaves) — with the tranche id as the asset and face as the quantity at 1. Market paper is delivered to the region's CLEARING_HOUSE (the hub the register's fills draw down, W2) and a facility to its lending BANK; retirements run the same wires backwards. Operations: `issueTranche` (issuer → holder, the row appended WITH the wire that created it — `TrancheStore.wireRef`, and `pushLadderRow` now demands a wire number: a ladder row with no wire does not compile), `retireTranche` (holder → issuer, face off the row; retiring more than the row carries is a defect), `commitLadder` (the chain re-linked; a row dropped with face still on it is a defect — paper cannot leave a ladder without a wire), `rebuildLadder` (a merger's consolidation: every current row retired, every new one issued), `moveFacilityLender` (a resolved bank's facilities to the assuming bank, one wire each, lender to lender), `seedLadder` (a seeded or born firm's ladder, no wires — B's gap). The store is sealed (`ReadonlyTrancheStore` on `V2World.tranches`; hygiene rule 5 extended to `engine2/tranches`' mutators). **Writers routed:** stage 08's kernel (the liquidity revolver, the accretive call and its replacement issue, every row maturing this week — all of them, where the old filter dropped any second maturing row silently — the term-out's bridges, the primary's tranche, the withdrawn-refinancing revolver, the maintenance bridges, prepayments, opportunistic deleveraging, the write-back), 07f's commercial paper (matured paper retired, the roll placed, the failed roll's revolver), the overdraft sweep and 02b's overdraft conversion, the merger's consolidation, bank resolution's re-key, and births/seed through `seedLadder`. **One wire per placement:** the ladder's issue IS the primary's asset wire (issuer → house, the whole deal), so 07b/07d/07f's `primaryAssetOf` legs are gone and the lead's residual now comes off the house (house → lead) instead of the issuer — the house receives the deal, the fills take the market's share, the lead the rest. **The register side settles through the house too:** the paying agent's retirements and placements and the merger's exchange are wires holder ↔ CLEARING_HOUSE (they were holder ↔ issuer, so the issuer's paper was counted twice against the ladder's own wire — the first trace's 490 issuers off by 0.02B each). **The W3 audit line** — `lastWires.issuerNetUSDByKey`: face issued by a region's companies minus face retired to them, per region and kind, must equal the ladders' change (`snapshot.ladderUSDByKey`) — prints from week 4 at 0.00–0.35B per region-kind: births (a born firm's ladder is seeded — B) and dead firms' estates (a written-off claim leaves the register through the estate while the dead ladder stands — W6); `LADDER_TRACE=1` prints the per-issuer decomposition with NEW/GONE/DEFAULTED tags. **The per-tranche register is deferred into P** (recorded in §5-WIRES): while the books clear per issuer, keying the register by tranche is pro-rata bookkeeping with no price behind it; P's per-instrument clearing keys the fills by tranche by construction. **Measured, 13 weeks:** every macro line of the week table and every M/O/P/X/F line identical to §7.368's; W1 no line; W2 as before (the non-register holders); W3 as above (10 weeks, worst 0.35B); 124 violations against 114, the ten W3 lines.
370. **WIRES W4 — THE GOODS LEDGER: EVERY MOVE OF GOODS A WIRE, THE STOCK IDENTITY CLOSED (2026-09-02).** `src/engine/ledger/goods-ledger.ts`: every move of goods between two parties is a wire (kind GOOD, the sub-unit as the asset, units as the quantity at the unit price the move was struck at) — `deliverGoods`; what is not a move is a TRANSFORMATION on the same journal (`produceGoods`, `consumeGoods`, `scrapGoods`, and `mintedUnits` where a seller's settlement clamped a negative stock — recorded so the identity closes and named by its own line, "W4 no unit sold that did not exist", which prints nothing). The lot store is sealed (`ReadonlyLotStore`; `pushLot` demands the wire that delivered the lot — `receiveInputLot`), a firm's finished stock is written by the ledger only (`settleOutputInventory` with the stage's own association order, `setOutputStock`, `moveOutputUnits`, `scrapOutputUnitsTo`; hygiene rule 5 extended to `engine2/lots`' mutators and to `outputInventoryBySubUnit` writes). **Who holds what:** a NAMED carrier holds a consignment while it moves — seller → carrier at dispatch, one consignment per carrier by the lane's shares, carrier → buyer at arrival (`InTransitShipment.carrierTicker/carrierRegion`); a household, a treasury and a segment pool are SOURCES AND SINKS, never holders (a pool sells from no stock and consumes what it buys; the transport pool's carriage on lanes no fleet serves passes through it the same way — measured: counting the pool as a holder booked its own electricity purchases as carriage, 64k units); a machine or an operating purchase is consumed on receipt (plant, not stock); a firm supplying itself moves nothing between parties (`INTERNAL_MOVE`). The kernels' FIFO draw reports what it actually took (`takenUnits`, residues under 0.0001u go with the lot) into a front lane (`F.inputUnitsConsumed`, zeroed on scratch reuse — unreset, week 2 carried week 1's draw), recorded per region and sub-unit after the pass. **The W4 audit line** — per region and sub-unit, in units, Δ(finished stock + input lots + a named carrier's consignments) = produced + minted − consumed − scrapped + wires in − wires out — closes: seven weeks exactly, then floating-point dust (−0.1u on flows of 10⁶); its instruments are `GOODS_TRACE=1` (the decomposition of the worst gaps: stock by part, transformations, wires in/out, lot receipts, per-firm and per-holder-kind) and the harness's `[front-trace]` line. **Measured, 13 weeks:** the money family CLEAN — "the money that is not anyone's" 0.00B on 0 lines (M6's one line gone); O1 sovereign once (34.36B, HEAD's line), O2 market cap once (0.02B), O4/O5/P/X/F as before (O5 "shipments" counts consignment RECORDS, now one per carrier, so its count rose from 2,262 to 11,498 with the same goods — the check should count units; left as is, noted); W1 nothing, W3 as before, W4 dust only. **Declared re-baseline (§7.313):** the week table diverges from §7.369's at the last displayed digit from week 4 and the price index at week 13 reads 214 against 189 (unemployment and GDP agree to the digit). Traced to a 1e-8 difference at WEEK 1 in three firms (two industrials' input cost by 0.1 USD, one bank's reserves by 0.004 USD) that the chaotic dynamics amplify; experiments ruled out the per-carrier split (one consignment per lot drifts the same), the wires and the records (all switched off, the same three firms differ), the party-interning order (the sink pass moved after the money legs, no change) and the arithmetic (the stage's association order restored); the source is in the structural rewrite of stage 05's stock settlement or the estate's inventory path and is NOT pinned — §6 carries it. The bisection was stopped on the user's instruction (2026-09-02: harness runs are too costly; minimize them) — the run cost, not the finding, closed the search.
371. **WIRES W6 (= B) — BIRTH AND DEATH ARE WIRES (2026-09-02).** **Birth:** the generator sketches a private newborn with a debt base nobody lent it; `fundNewbornDebt` (pe-lifecycle.ts) makes that debt what a private newborn's debt is — a FACILITY from its home bank, priced off its own default probability at the bank's hurdle (`facilityMarginBpsFor`), issued by wire (`issueTranche`, issuer → BANK), booked on the bank as a loan through the same credit event every facility uses, its proceeds paid to the firm (BANK_CREDIT → COMPANY, 'firm birth: facility proceeds'); the pool's carve-out is the founders' remainder of the opening balance, so the newborn's cash is what the generator intended and nothing appears on a ladder from nowhere; core.ts no longer seeds a born firm's ladder; an FDI subsidiary is born with no debt (FDI is equity) and an empty ladder. **Death:** the estate retires the dead ladder as it settles — `retireLadderFace` (tranche-ledger.ts: face of one KIND off the issuer's rows pro rata, the estate's claims being per holder and kind) on every recovery and write-off, register claims moving holder → CLEARING_HOUSE (the register side, W2's `transferHolding`, no longer holder → issuer) with the ladder retiring against the house, facilities retiring bank → issuer beside the bank's own write-off; a closed estate extinguishes whatever face no claim covered (`rebuildLadder` to empty, 'estate closed: ladder extinguished'). **Measured, 13 weeks (the one run):** the W3 line CLOSES — no week prints it (births and dead estates were its only sources, §7.369) — W1 and W4 print nothing, the money family is clean (0 lines), O1/O2/O4/O5/P/X/F exactly §7.370's; W2 stays open on the non-register holders; 115 violations against 131. Declared re-baseline: the week table diverges from §7.370's from week 4 (a dead firm's ladder now shrinks with its estate, so its debt, its interest and O1's "outstanding" read differently — real economics, not dust). **Still seeded without wires** (B's remainder): the world's week-0 positions and a newborn's plant and product lines — the seed is the initial condition, not an event; a newborn's plant is a founders' contribution in kind with no wire yet.
372. **WIRES D, FIRST SLICE — MARKET CAP AND TOTAL DEBT ARE READS (2026-09-02).** `Company.marketCap` and `Company.totalDebt` are gone; `marketCapOf` (price × shares, zero for a firm nobody can buy) and `totalDebtOf` (the week-end ladder view) / `ladderTotalUSD` (the live rows, mid-week) replace their eighteen writers and ~40 readers; `companyFairValuePerShare`/`companyBookEquityUSD` take the caller's mid-week face (stage 08 passes its fresh total); the generator's cohort lifts move the shares and the tranche face instead of the two stored sums; O2's "market cap = price × shares" check is deleted (a read cannot disagree with its parts); hygiene forbids a `.marketCap`/`.totalDebt` read outside the row store and the kernel lanes. **The store's two lanes are DERIVED COLUMNS** (`DERIVED_F64_FIELDS` in company-store.ts, one `derivedColumn` derivation) at every fill — refresh, per-row sync, the field mesh, the checker — because the first run showed what a stored copy of a derivation does when its source dies: `syncCompanyField(state, 'totalDebt' | 'marketCap')` in core.ts read the deleted field as `undefined`, wrote NaN into the lanes, the front kernel's Tobin's Q and effective debt rate went NaN, 6,608 product lines printed NaN by week 3, and the equity book died with −2.09B "with no owner". **Two latent defects that run uncovered, both fixed:** (i) 07e charged a book holder the auction had NOT admitted (an index fund with nothing investable is dropped by `indexFundsForBook`) as a seller of everything and dropped its rows — money paid for shares that vanished; a non-participant's register now passes through at the cleared marks and pays nothing. (ii) A BANK drew a maintenance bridge: the bridge is the house bank's credit and a bank has none, so pre-W3 it was a facility with no lender (paper with no holder, interest to no one — the O4 "facilities on ladders = loans on banks" disease); W3's ledger refused it at week 12 (`WORG-MAINT-13 names no lending bank`). `bridgeCapacityUSD` (capital-programme.ts) is now investment grade AND `hasHouseBank`; a bank funds its upkeep from its own cash. **Measured, 13 weeks:** the run completes (the first D run died at week 3, the second at week 12); no NaN anywhere; W1/W3/W4 print nothing, W2 stays open on the non-register holders; O2's line is gone by construction; O1 once (USA, week 6), O4/O5/P/X/F the same families as §7.371; 126 violations against 115, the surplus being this seed's later trajectory (a week-12 merger — O3's rows on the absorbed firm and N2's re-key name, both 2/13 — and two banks, WORG and OOCO, running a negative margin at week 13). **OPEN — THE MONEY FAMILY REGAINED A LINE (§6):** M6 prints from week 8 in EUR (3.07B unexplained, rising to 3.61B by week 13) and from week 11 in UK (2.30–2.32B), nearly constant week on week, and "the money that is not anyone's" reads 5.93B across those two lines against 0.00B at §7.371. Ruled out from the code without a run: every D read is semantics-preserving (the eighteen writers wrote price × shares and the ladder's sum), the two fixes above move no money, the prime-brokerage draw is booked as the broker's own account (counted), and the merger closes four weeks after the onset. Not ruled out: a money-creating flow the M6 decomposition does not list that this trajectory's higher rates (10Y 6.75 against 4.40) make material — the gap's constancy reads like an interest-sized weekly flow. NOT diagnosed here on the user's directive (two runs already spent); the NEXT commit's one 13-week run carries `BANK_IDENTITY_TRACE=1 CB_IDENTITY_TRACE=1`, which costs nothing extra and names the stage. **Harness cost note (the user's directive):** two 13-week runs were spent on this commit, not one — the first died at week 12 on defect (ii), which no cheap gate can reach (it needs a bank whose upkeep outruns its operating cash, week 12 of this seed). **D's remainder** (§5-WIRES D): the bank's loan book against its loans, the region's deposits against the banks' lines, the institution's `totalAssetsUSD` against its rows.
373. **WIRES D, SECOND SLICE — THE BANK'S LOAN BOOKS ARE READS OF ITS LOANS; THE M6 LINE WAS THE AUDIT'S (2026-09-02).** `BankingSector.businessLoanBookUSD` and `consumerLoanBookUSD` are gone; `businessLoanBookOf` (Σ `businessLoans`), `consumerLoanBookOf` (Σ `householdLoans`) and `loanBooksOf` (domain/banking.ts) replace their eleven writers and ~40 readers (the settlement's credit events, the sweep's SME rows, the estate's write-offs, the weekly lending passes, the bank transfer, the RWA and leverage reads, the M5 identity, the trace, four UI pages, the harness's capital line). The REGIONAL AGGREGATE carries no rows and now no loan book of its own: `regionLoanBooksUSD` sums the named banks' rows wherever a region's book is wanted (`evolveBankingSector` takes it as an input from its caller; the settlement's incremental patch of the aggregate's scalar is deleted — there is no second copy to keep in step; the UI's region pages sum the banks). The seed's stated loan-to-GDP ratios (`BANK_BALANCE_SHEET_RATIOS`, a stated number for R's registry) survive only as `seedLoanBookUSD`/`seedLoanBookShareUSD`, read by the three places the seed SIZES something off them and never stored: a bank's opening revenue (the generator), the opening funding side before the migrations, and the consumer scalar HH3's real pools replace (its stand-in in the prior RWA, so the seed's equity arithmetic is unchanged). **The §7.372 M6 line was not the engine's.** Read from the code: `depositsOf` in the money audit counted the clients' margin line (`clientMarginUSD`, the FX desks' margin received — fx-hedging.ts) while the audit SNAPSHOT's `bankDepositsUSD` did not, so M6 compared a week-end stock WITH the margin against a week-start stock WITHOUT it and the whole margin STOCK printed as "unexplained" every week the desks held any — EUR 3.07→3.61B from week 8, UK 2.30B from week 11, the constant, rates-linked, region-specific signature §7.372 could not name. One `depositsOf` now lives in domain/banking.ts and both reads take it. **A second latent defect, named by the conservation trace (`EQ_CONS_TRACE=1`, one 10-week run):** the clearing engine's shard merge dropped any filled position of one UNIT or less from the holdings matrix (`filledUSD > 1`) — a dollar of dust on the credit books, but the equity book clears in SHARES, and 0.44 shares of UK_YQHL at $279,800 (the §7.259 class: units read as dollars) was $124k the holder was paid for by nobody; the UK session's owner check caught it at week 9. The threshold is now in the book's MONEY (units × the cleared price where the stat is a price). **Measured, 13 weeks (the run after the two fixes; three 13-week runs and one traced 10-week run were spent on this commit — the first died at week 9 on the dust defect, which no cheap gate reaches):** the money family is CLEAN again — M6 prints no line, "the money that is not anyone's" reads 0.00B across 0 lines against §7.372's 5.93B — W1/W3/W4 print nothing, W2 stays open on the non-register holders, no NaN, no defect; O1 3/13, O3/N2 2/13 (this seed's week-12 merger again), O4/O5/P/X/F the same families; 125 violations against 126. The §6 row "THE M6 EUR/UK LINE" is closed. **D's remainder:** the region's deposits against the banks' lines (the aggregate's other scalars), the institution's `totalAssetsUSD` against its rows. Then N.
374. **WIRES D, THIRD SLICE — AN INSTITUTION'S TOTAL ASSETS ARE A READ (2026-09-02).** `InstitutionalEntity.totalAssetsUSD` is gone. `institutionTotalAssetsUSD` (domain/institutions.ts: cash + what it is owed + overnight cash lent + the stock-loan book + the register's rows, or a sponsor's portfolio at the public comparable) is the formula; the stage read (`institutionTotalAssetsUSD(ctx, e)`, institutional-balance-sheet.ts) walks the register's rows live and leaves the receivable out while the clearing store is live (the rows are then the week's OPENING register and the books' cash legs sit in the receivable — the opening book, not a half-settled one); the closed-state read (`institutionTotalAssetsFromState`) serves the UI and the harness; the seed reads its own itemized rows (`seedInstitutionTotalAssetsUSD`). `markInstitutionalBooks` — the week-end mark every sizing pass read a WEEK STALE — is deleted, its `run('institutional-marking')` with it; the estate's `totalAssetsUSD -= loss` is gone (the rows moved, the read follows); the six `entityRequiredReturn` callers and the three class-budget callers pass the live read (a pension's hurdle divides by it); the seed's PE limited-partner weights and the manager's AUM read the seed's rows; ~40 readers converted; hygiene forbids `.totalAssetsUSD` outside the two read modules. Declared re-baseline: every structural pool (07b–07f, the ETF appetite, the FX speculator cap, the prime-brokerage line, the swap duration gap, the money-fund fee split) now sizes off THIS week's opening book instead of last week's close. **Measured, 13 weeks (ONE run, shared with §7.375 on the user's cost directive — the two changes are independent in code, the seed split and the read, so the scoreboard is attributed to both and confounded for neither):** the run completes; the money family is clean, "the money that is not anyone's" 0.00B across 0 lines, W1/W3/W4 print nothing, W2 open on the non-register holders, no NaN, no defect; O1 1/13, O3/N2 2/13 (the seed's merger), O4/O5/P/F the same families, X down to X1 alone (3/13); 115 violations against §7.373's 125. **D is COMPLETE** for the three quantities §5-WIRES D named (market cap and total debt §7.372, the loan books §7.373, total assets here); the regional banking aggregate's remaining scalars (deposits, reserves, equity, the desk views — twelve readers) are the sum 02b already rebuilds them as and stay as a §5 remainder for A (one account kind makes the aggregate a query). Next: N.
375. **INS — THREE INSURERS PER REGION, NOT ONE (user, 2026-09-02: "just one is TBTF and has no competition").** bootstrap/firms.ts: the sector's 0.42 institutional slice is split across `INSURERS_PER_REGION = 3` insurers on the same firm-size curve (`FIRM_CONCENTRATION_DECAY`) every other cohort is generated from — the asset managers' (§7 three houses) and hedge funds' (HF1) precedent: a split, not an addition, so the region's institutional pool is unchanged and no new concentration number exists. Everything downstream was already plural: the insurance stage splits the premium pool pro rata by the insurers' capital and pays each by name, the household claims and the estate machinery are per entity, the seed sizes each insurer's book by its own share. What competition buys: a policy can move, a price can be undercut, and one insurer's failure is a resolution among peers rather than the sector's. **Measured:** the §7.374 run — the three insurers per region clear, insure and are paid by name through the same stage; nothing in the scoreboard names an insurer.
376. **WIRES N, FIRST SLICE — THE DATED WIRE, AND CORPORATE TAX AS ITS FIRST CARRIER (2026-09-02).** The payment journal carries a `settleWeek` column beside the wire's (settlement.ts: `journalAppendRow`/`journalPush` take it; `pay`/`payByIds` accept `settleWeek` and default to the wire journal's week — `settlementWeek()`, the one clock). A settlement pass settles the rows due at or before its week (`rowDue`) and CARRIES the rest into the fresh journal — the same rows, the same wire numbers, settled by the pass of their own week; a dated row counts against nobody's running net (`addPending`/`applyPendingLeg` skip it) and not against the close's balance (the overdraft sweep skips it); the shards and the worker ship the column (`journalSettle`). **Corporate tax:** the walk's weekly accrual is now `post(CORPORATE_TAX_REASON, −accrual, GOVERNMENT, true, quarterEndWeek)` — a wire to the treasury dated at the quarter's end (`post` journals a dated leg without touching the walk's running cash: an obligation is not this week's money); the quarter's own week is due at once. `Company.accruedTaxLiabilityUSD` is gone — what a firm has accrued and not paid is `undueOwedByPayerUSD` over its undue rows (the UI's tax line reads it off the state's carried journal); the treasury's weekly corporate receipt is `dueToPayeeUSD` over the rows falling due (11-fiscal), so `ctx.taxCollectedByRegion` and the walk's `collectUSD` capture are deleted (the accrual capture stays for the shard replay's float identity). Declared re-baseline: the twelve earlier weeks' accruals now leave the firm's DEPOSIT at the quarter's settlement rather than its cash walk, and the firm's spendable balance never carries them. **Measured, 13 weeks (two runs on this commit):** the first printed ONE new line — F2 "revenue = tax remitted" at the quarter week in JPN (revenue 5.70B against 8.12B remitted) — because the carried rows due at the quarter settle at the MID-WEEK pass and were gone from the journal when 11-fiscal counted what fell due; the receipt now reads the report's settled treasury flows for the week plus the rows still due for the close, and the second run is clean of it: the money family clean, "the money that is not anyone's" 0.00B, W1/W3/W4 print nothing, no NaN, no defect, 115 violations — §7.374's scoreboard exactly, the same families and counts. **N's remainder** (§5-WIRES): money in transit (the households' T+1 hand-off, `pendingBankSettlementUSD`), the unfunded deficit (`pendingUnfundedDeficitUSD` — the central bank's advance drawn and repaid by wire), the holders' accrued coupons (`pendingHolderAccrualUSD`, which re-keys with the paper and so lands with P's per-tranche register); the intra-stage holder batching (`pendingHolderSettlements`/`pendingHolderCashUSD`) survives no week and is a queue, not state.
377. **WIRES A, FIRST SLICE — THE ACCOUNT STORE, AS A MIRROR WITH A GATE (2026-09-02).** `src/engine/ledger/accounts.ts`: one row per (party, bank) — a company's deposit at its house bank, a bank's own reserves at the central bank (its COMPANY party settles on the same row), the treasury's net position there (account less the advance drawn), the household sector's balance at each of its region's banks plus the slice still in transit (HH4d's T+1), a pool's balance at each bank by market share, the credit-creation and securities voids at each bank, the clearing houses and the central banks' issuance as rows at nobody — and ONE rule for a settled row (`applySettledRow`): payer −a, payee +a, and where a row sits at a bank that bank's own reserve row moves by the same amount (reserves leave the payer's bank and arrive at the payee's); a row at the central bank IS the central-bank side and carries no second leg; a row at nobody carries none. A sector party splits a leg across its rows by a stated split (households: all to transit today, the banks by share at A2; pools: the market shares settlement already uses). **The method is W1's:** the store is REBUILT from the balance fields before every settlement pass (`buildAccountMirror`), the pass applies every settled row to it beside the legacy nine-way writes, and `compareToBooks` proves the two agree after the pass — every company's cash, every entity's, every pool's and household sector's total, every bank's reserves, every treasury's net position — as **M7 accounts = books** and **M7 every settled row has an account** (`accountMismatchUSD`, `accountMismatchWorst`, `accountRowsUnmapped` on the settlement summary). A two-week probe printed nothing on either. **Measured, 13 weeks (the one run, after a two-week probe):** M7 prints NOTHING — after every pass of every week the store's one rule reproduces every company's cash, every entity's, every pool's and household sector's total, every bank's reserves and every treasury's net position to the dollar, and every settled row found its party's row; the money family clean, "the money that is not anyone's" 0.00B, W1/W3/W4 nothing, no NaN, no defect, 115 violations — §7.376's scoreboard exactly. The nine-way switch is therefore ALREADY one rule in disguise, proven; A2 can replace it. **A's next slices:** A2 the store persists on the world and becomes the AUTHORITY — settlement writes it and projects the fields (one writer), the household legs land on the banks by share at once (the T+1 transit and `pendingBankSettlementUSD` die, N's money-in-transit with them, the CB identity's deferred term with it), the pools' per-bank rows are the banks' SME lines; A3 the readers flip (`balanceOf`) and the fields die per kind — `cash`, `cashUSD`, `cashReservesUSD`, the five deposit lines, the TGA — the regional aggregate's scalars (D's remainder) become Σ rows; A4 settlement's switch is deleted.
378. **WIRES A, SECOND SLICE — THE STORE IS THE AUTHORITY OF THE PASS; THE HOUSEHOLDS' MONEY IN TRANSIT IS GONE (2026-09-02).** Settlement applies every settled row to the account store and PROJECTS the books from it (`projectBooks`, accounts.ts): a company's cash is its row, an entity's and a pool's their rows, the household sector's its rows at the banks, a bank's reserves its own row, a bank's four deposit lines move by its depositors' rows by class, and the treasury's account and the central bank's advance are the two signs of its net position (§5-CLOSE M4's rule in one place). The nine-way switch keeps only its TALLIES (the report the M family and 02b read); every balance write it made, the bank-sheet rebuild and the treasury block are deleted. **The household sector's legs land on its region's banks by market share at once** — the split the pools' legs always had — and the banks' reserves follow in the same pass: HH4d's T+1 hand-off is gone (`pendingBankSettlementUSD` deleted from the household state, the bank pass's `priorHouseholdEtfPurchasesUSD` parameter and its deposit/cash posting deleted, the report's `householdDeferredUSD` always zero, the CB identity's in-transit term, M1's, M3's, M6's and the snapshot's `householdInTransitUSD` deleted, the harness's two views likewise) — which closes N's "money in transit" by construction. Equity is not a balance: the bank's own-account legs are its income and expense and stay the pass's. A two-week probe: M clean. **Measured, 13 weeks (the one run, after a two-week probe):** the money family clean — M1 closes WITHOUT an in-transit term, M3's household line equals the sector's deposits with no pending term, M6 explains the stock with no transit, M7 nothing — "the money that is not anyone's" 0.00B, W1/W3/W4 nothing, no NaN, no defect; 124 violations against 115: the same O/P/F/N families, X1 (a solvent bank at a negative margin) 7/13 against 3/13 and one X2 week — the trajectory, not the ledger: the households' money reaches the banks a week earlier, so every bank's reserves and deposit lines shift by a week's household flow. Declared re-baseline. **A3 next:** the store persists on the world and every reader takes `balanceOf`; the fields die per kind (`cash`, `cashUSD`, `cashReservesUSD`, the five deposit lines, the treasury's two); the regional aggregate's scalars become Σ rows (D's remainder); A4 deletes the switch and its tallies become reads of the rows' deltas.
379. **HANDOFF (2026-09-02, end of the WIRES session).** HEAD `702d26a` on `claude/master-plan-review-u0y2q6` and `main`, tree clean; twelve commits this session (§7.367–378, INS §7.375). **The state of the campaign** is §5-WIRES's status lines and §4's HANDOVER block; the scoreboard to judge against is §7.378's. **What the successor starts on — A3.1, companies' cash as a read of the account store.** The survey (done, not built): `.cash` is READ at 73 sites in 25 files (02b 8, estate-resolution 6, initialization 5, audit/money 4, stage08-back/pe-lifecycle/labor-market/FDI/10-mergers/07f 3 each, the rest 1–2) and WRITTEN at eight — `balance.ts:67` (the ledger's own), `accounts.ts:228` (A2's projection), `stage08-back.ts:2018` (the walk's `{...comp, cash: cash.usd}` snapshot for the board's fair value — a READ of the walk, keep as a parameter), and five zeroings at a party's birth or death (`10-mergers.ts:135` spin-off, `pe-lifecycle.ts:818`, `bank-resolution.ts:187`, `foreign-direct-investment.ts:140`, and the seed). The walk's `cash.usd` is the firm's INTRA-WEEK view (starts from the lane, moves by every leg the walk posts) and stays; only the persisted `Company.cash` goes. Design: (1) `v2.accounts` persists — rows keyed by the party KEY interned in v2 (`internString(v2, partyKey(ref))`), `rowsOfParty` rebuilt from keys on load (party ids are process-local); (2) `buildAccountMirror` takes company rows FROM the persistent store (opening = current balance) and keeps mirroring the other kinds from their fields; (3) `projectBooks` stops writing `comp.cash`; (4) the seed opens each company's account where it wrote `cash`; births open an account by wire (W6's newborn proceeds already arrive by payment — the row opens at zero), deaths close it (the estate's last wire empties it, `closeAccount` asserts zero); (5) every reader takes `cashOf(v2, company)` = the party's rows (a bank's COMPANY party is its reserve row, which is what §7.377's mirror already does), the UI included; (6) `Company.cash` is deleted, the store lane `cash` becomes a derived column (`DERIVED_F64_FIELDS`, §7.372's pattern), hygiene's derived rule gains `.cash\b`. Expected: F1 closes by construction (the statement reads the ledger). One 2-week probe, one 13-week run. **Then, per kind:** entities' `cashUSD` (76 reads), the pools' `cashUSD`, the household sector's `depositsUSD` (67 reads share the name with the banks' line — split the name first), the treasury's `treasuryAccountUSD`/`waysAndMeansUSD` (28; the unfunded deficit becomes the advance by wire, N's remainder), and last the bank lines (`cashReservesUSD` 63 and the four deposit classes) once 02b's evolve, bank-lending's funding split, `absorbBankSheet` and the estates are account operations. **Not done and why:** N's coupon accruals (`pendingHolderAccrualUSD`) re-key with the paper and land with P; R and P untouched; INS has its split but not its price competition (§5-INS). **Costs this session:** 21 harness runs of 13 weeks and four short probes/traces across twelve commits — two commits needed a second run for a defect only a quarter week or a week-12 bank shows (§7.372, §7.376); the two-week probe before the one run is the cheapest insurance and is now the practice.
380. **THE TIMED READ AT HANDOFF (2026-09-02, `PROFILE=1 WEEKS=4`, one measured week after three of warm-up, this container).** Weeks 1–4 cost 4,446 / 6,070 / 8,642 / 9,095 ms — rising every week, the state-growth drift of §7.335 in a new tree (the contract book, the register and the journals all grow). Where the 9.1 s goes: **07i-commodity-futures 2,253 ms (25%)**, **fx-hedging 1,698 ms (19%)**, **05-unit-bidding 1,383 ms (16%)**, **08-company-fundamentals 1,142 ms (13%)** — four stages are 73% of the week; then etf-flows 419 (5%), 07h-cds-clearing 324 (4%), settlement 215 (2%), holdings-writeback 185 (2%), 07b 160, securities-lending 141, fx-clearing 110, 07d 105, 07g 96, 07f 90, 07e 74; everything else under 60 ms. The two derivative books (futures, FX hedging) at 44% of the week are the surprise: neither is a goods or credit book, both were built as one contract layer (§7.337), and both scale with the contract book's rows (§7.328) — the first place to look is a per-contract walk that should be per-instrument. A 13-week harness is ~5 minutes on this box. Recorded as FINALIZATION step 0 (a profile is not a run: `PROFILE=1 WEEKS=4` costs 30 s and is allowed at any time).
381. **THE FULL READ AT HANDOFF (2026-09-02, user: "read the entire master plan from top to bottom to confirm; SCALE and Aurora are fine outside").** Every section read in order, §1 through §7.380. Confirmed: nothing half-built stands outside FINALIZATION except what the user parked — SCALE (§5-SCALE, resume point in its banner) and Aurora's remainders (§5-AU) — and the §6.2 watchlist, which is a watchlist. Corrected on the read, all text, no code: the §1.10 ladder now states §1.25's cadence (a 30 s profile any time, a 2-week probe before the one run, ONE 13-week run per commit, the 60-week reference at the end); the §2.1 pipeline table lists the live stage order (`overdraft-sweep`, `bank-funding-close`, `settlement-funding` and `register-consolidation` in; `institutional-marking` out, §7.374); §2.4 no longer names a boundary line (CLOSE C3 deleted the party, §7.351 — the harness runs the audit families instead), counts 123 tests, the 354 lint ceiling (349 measured), and the hygiene budgets as the script holds them (2 / 16 / 54), and maps Aurora as the product UI with the bench at `#bench`. Not added as steps, with reason: DIST-P's open blocks and BRAINS' "not taken" items are stated numbers, which FINALIZATION step 12 (R, the registry) covers generically — a separate step per constant would be the list R exists to replace.
382. **FINALIZATION STEP 0, THE DERIVATIVES HALF — ONE STAGE, ONE STANDING INDEX; BYTE-IDENTICAL AT 4 AND 13 WEEKS; THE WEEK 9.0 → 5.5 s (2026-09-02).** The cost §7.380 named was legible by reading: every market asked the live book the same two questions — a party's standing cover on one side of one class (§7.241) and its PFE charge against the one desk budget — and each asked by WALKING THE WHOLE BOOK PER PARTICIPANT with a party-key string built per contract per walk (a producer, a consumer, a fund, an invoice holder, a desk per commodity per tenor), while the book carried 67,181 live futures at week 4 (every long paired with every seller, per commodity, per tenor, per week — §7.328's growth) and 80,398 at week 14; the futures desk charge also rebuilt a 4,000-firm rating map per call, and the strike walk ran a linear `find` over ~2,000 participants per fill. **Built:** `domain/derivatives/standing-book.ts` — the book folded ONCE, in book order (so every sum is the float the walk produced), into cover per (class, side, party, reference, tenor) with the four wildcard shapes the questions take, and the PFE charge per party at the graded add-on and at the flat one; `standingBookOf` (derivative-lifecycle.ts) memoises it on the week's context keyed by the book ARRAY — the lifecycle and a resolution's re-key replace the array when a contract leaves (rebuild), a strike appends (`extend` folds the tail) — so the clearing phase builds it twice and the post-settlement phase once. `stages/derivatives.ts` is THE stage: one market view per phase, every class in `DERIVATIVE_CLASS_IDS` order (the registry's, new), at its declared phase (CLEARING with the books; POST_SETTLEMENT for the forwards, which hedge what settlement left behind), settled through the one lifecycle before the market (a rate leg pays what printed) or after it (a mark leg needs the print the market makes); `stages/derivative-markets/{irs,cds,commodity-future,fx-forward}.ts` behind `DERIVATIVE_MARKETS` (compile-loud) carry what is each class's own — the moved code with the walks replaced by lookups, the futures fill's `find` by a map, the FX dealer pick over a per-region list. `07g`, `07h`, `07i` and `fx-hedging` are deleted; `deskStandingPfeChargeUSD` (fx-clearing's read) answers from the index.
