One rule where there were two: the FX desk's standing charge read every class at its FLAT add-on while the CDS and futures desks read the graded one (§7.341(b)'s site the fix missed); every desk reads the graded charge now — and the 4- and 13-week dumps say it moved nothing in this world (why not — no bank on an investment-grade reference, or an FX desk whose capacity never binds — was not traced; the dumps are the gate, and the next world that differs here differs for the right reason). **Gates:** tsc, lint 349/354, hygiene, 124 tests (the index against the walks on a mixed book, and after a strike); `STATE_DUMP` differ IDENTICAL at week 4 and at week 13 against the same-day HEAD baselines, stripped logs identical, the scoreboard §7.378's exactly (124 violations / 26 families). **Measured (`PROFILE=1 WEEKS=4`, this box):** the week 9,014 → 5,478 ms; `07g+07h+07i` 81+267+2,222 = 2,570 → `derivatives` 573; `fx-hedging` 1,604 → `derivatives-post-settlement` 339; weeks 1–4 4.6/5.7/5.7/5.7 s against 5.1/6.4/9.2/9.2 — the week-on-week climb §7.380 read as state growth was mostly these walks over the growing book; the 13-week run's weeks read 4.6 → 6.6 s against 5.6 → 11.8 s. What the derivative stages still cost is ∝ the live contracts (the settle walk marks and pays every futures contract weekly — real legs, 67k of them), a representation question for the step's own remainder, not a walk. Step 0 continues with 05 and 08.
383. **STEP 0 CONTINUED — THE TWO WALKS THE LINE PROFILE NAMED AFTER THE MERGE, BOTH EXACT: THE WEEK 5.5 → 4.4 s (2026-09-02, commit 19ab38c).** A `--cpu-prof` over the esbuild bundle (§7.300's method, 4 weeks, self time by source line) on the §7.382 tree put two functions at the top: **(a) `debitRow` in the holdings ledger — 12.4% of the profile, one caller: `applyPendingCorporateActionSettlements`.** A debit walked the holder's whole chain, built a `kept` list of every row and RELINKED the whole chain on every hit — four walks and an allocation per action — while the corporate-action pass debits every holder per instrument every week over a register of 150k rows (week 13). Now one walk lands the debit and notes whether any row has to leave (the debited one emptied, or a residue elsewhere on the book, the same test as before); only then is the chain relinked, else the book is marked synced and dirty — exactly what relinking an unchanged chain did besides relinking. **(b) the standing index's own keys (§7.382) — 4.7%:** four ~45-character composite strings per side per contract, 560k map probes per build over a 70k-contract book, three builds a week. The index is a party's array of its eight (class, side) books now, each with its totals by reference and by tenor — one party probe and three short-key probes per side, every sum still in book order; the flat-charge read nobody called is gone. **Gates:** tsc, lint 349/354, hygiene, 124 tests; `STATE_DUMP` differ IDENTICAL at week 4 and at week 13 against the same-day HEAD baselines (the §7.382 tree's dumps are the same bytes), stripped logs identical, scoreboard 124 / 26. **Measured (`PROFILE=1 WEEKS=4`):** the week 5,478 → 4,443 ms; 08-company-fundamentals 1,175 → 745 (the relink); `derivatives` 573 → 145 and `derivatives-post-settlement` 339 → 132 (the keys); weeks 1–4 4.0/4.5/4.5/4.9 s; the 13-week run's weeks 4.0 → 5.7 s. Against §7.380: 9,014 → 4,443 ms/week at week 4, both commits byte-identical. What the profile names next, each a representation the parked SCALE programme owns: 05-unit-bidding's per-row emission floor (§7.328), the front seam and the back kernel's object reads (§7.317), the register's chain walks in the corporate-action pass (a maintained (type, instrument) transpose, §7.332's disposition) — the honest remainder of step 0 waits on the user's go for §4.C, and this slice stops where §1.23 says a grind stops.
384. **WIRES A3.1 — A COMPANY'S CASH IS ITS ACCOUNT, THE FIELD IS GONE; BYTE-IDENTICAL AT 4 AND 13 WEEKS (2026-09-02).** Per §7.379's design. **Built:** `v2.accounts` (engine2/world.ts — one balance per party key interned in the world's string table, typed arrays and a Map, clone-safe) owned by `engine/ledger/accounts.ts`: `openAccount` (a second opening is a defect), `ensureAccount` (a party the store has no row for opens at ZERO on first sight — a newborn's balance arrives by payment, W6), `balanceOf`, `cashOf(v2, company)`; the pass mirror opens a company's row FROM the store and the projection writes the pass's result back INTO it; `Company.cash` is deleted (tsc named 70 sites in 35 files; the grep after found none cast-hidden), every reader takes `cashOf` — 02b, 05, 07e/07f, the kernel's board valuation (the valuation functions take cash explicitly: the account, or stage 08's mid-week walk), labour, estates, mergers (`checkForMerger` takes the world), the paydown, the sweep, FDI, births, resolution, news, the audit's M3/M4/F1, the UI and the bench digest; the company row store's `cash` lane is a DERIVED column (§7.372's pattern, re-derived from the account at every fill and at the three `syncCompanyField` sites); hygiene fails a `<company>.cash` read by name. **The opening cash rides a STASH, not a field:** the generator's `cash:` literal became `stashOpeningCash(firm, usd)` beside the seed rings (`openingCashOf` reads it; the seed opens each account with it at assembly, a birth pays it) — the first cut carried it as a `GeneratedCompany` type stripped by a spread, which handed the state NEW objects and silently lost every firm's `WeakMap`-keyed ring stash (EUR unemployment 7.3 against 7.4 at week 2; found by the two-week probe, §1.25's cheapest insurance, paying for itself again). Deleted with the field: `creditHolderBalance` (no caller), the four birth/death zeroings (a spin-off's, a newborn's, a subsidiary's — their rows open at zero on first sight) but one: a resolved bank's shell keeps a transitional `resetAccount` to zero, because a bank's company row is the SEED's number that nothing ever moved (its goods-market self settles on its reserves) — **A3.1b** deletes that row and makes a bank's `cashOf` its reserves, the one declared change the design carries (§7.379) and the reason this commit could be exact: the bank read is unchanged here. **The dump keeps its `cash` column**, read off the account, so the differ still sees every balance. **Gates:** tsc, lint 349/354, hygiene, 124 tests; the two-week probe; `STATE_DUMP` differ IDENTICAL at week 4 and week 13 against the same-day HEAD baselines, stripped logs identical, scoreboard 124 / 26. **What the run says F1 is:** it prints the same 59 firms — the filed statement's cash line is the walk's mid-week view, the account is the settled balance, and they differ by the week's legs still in the journal; "closes by construction" (§7.379) was wrong, and the closing is a step of its own (the filing reads the account). M7's company line is a tautology now and was dropped from `compareToBooks`. Next: A3.1b, then A3.2 (entities).
385. **WIRES A3.2 — AN INSTITUTION'S CASH IS ITS ACCOUNT; BYTE-IDENTICAL AT 4 AND 13 WEEKS (2026-09-02).** §7.384's method, one kind on: `InstitutionalEntity.cashUSD` is deleted; `entityCashOf(v2, e)` reads the persistent row; the pass mirror opens an entity's row from the store and the projection writes back into it; the seed stashes each entity's opening cash beside it (its policy cash weight against its book plus the equity budget the issue could not absorb, §5-CLOSE O2) and opens the account at assembly; the three helpers that took an entity structurally (`institutionTotalAssetsUSD` in domain, `availablePurchaseCapacityUSD`/`stagePurchaseBudgetUSD`, `quoteMmfNetYieldAnnual`) take the cash explicitly, `indexFundsForBook` and `dryPowderUSD` take the world; ~45 sites in 30 files, the UI and the harness included; hygiene fails an entity's `.cashUSD` read by name. **Two instrument lessons, both the same lesson:** the harness's institutional-book conservation check read the PREVIOUS STATE OBJECT's cash — every week's state shares the one persistent store, so "last week's cash" was this week's and the check printed a 10% move that was not there; it snapshots the book values at each close now (§7.354's rule: an instrument comparing weeks keeps its own numbers, never a state object). And the harness's household report read `f.cashUSD` through an `any` cast — invisible to tsc and to the first hygiene name list; the sellable-shares line dropped 0.2B → 0.1B before the grep found it. Neither was the engine. **Gates:** tsc, lint 349/354, hygiene, 124 tests; two-week probe; `STATE_DUMP` differ IDENTICAL at week 4 and week 13 against the same-day HEAD baselines, the weekly counts and the scoreboard (124 / 26) identical, the sorted logs identical but for that check's own "before" (368.8B against the old 368.9B at week 5: the old read walked last week's entity OBJECTS, which the stages mutate in place, so its "before" already carried a slice of this week — the snapshot is the honest close; the engine is byte-identical). Next: A3.3, the pools.
386. **WIRES A3.3 — A POOL'S CASH IS ITS ROWS AT THE REGION'S BANKS, CARRIED; A BANK'S SME LINE IS THEIR SUM — A DECLARED RE-BASELINE (2026-09-02).** §7.378 had built the pools' per-bank rows by re-splitting each pool's total by THIS week's market shares at every pass and then moved the bank's SME line by the pass's per-bank delta — two derivations of one balance, and 02b re-strikes `bankMarketShare` from the deposit lines every week, so where a pool's money sat drifted from where its legs had landed. Now the rows are PERSISTENT (`v2.accounts.bankRowsByParty`: a sector party's row at each bank, `poolRowAt` opening at zero on first sight, `poolCashOf` the pool's sum, `smeDepositsAt` a bank's sum, `movePoolRowsToBank` when a bank is resolved or merged — called beside `absorbBankSheet` at both sites); the pass mirror opens a pool's rows FROM them, a leg still splits across the region's banks by share, the projection writes the rows back and then writes each bank's `smeDepositsUSD` AS their sum (one writer, no delta); `SmePool.cashUSD` is deleted (`poolCashOf` at every reader — the audit, labour, the sweep, births, the pool stage, 02b, the harness, the UI; hygiene fails a pool's `.cashUSD` read). **The seed opens the rows where it sized the lines:** the world (`v2`) is born at the top of `createInitialGameState` and carried by the state; each pool's row at each bank opens at its rounded share of the pool's opening cash (the stash, §7.384) and the bank's SME line is their sum — the line and the rows cannot disagree at week 0. **Gates:** tsc, lint 349/354, hygiene, 124 tests, the two-week probe; the one 13-week run: **115 violations / 24 families against §7.378's 124 / 26** — the money family CLEAN (M1–M7 print nothing, "the money that is not anyone's" 0.00B), the same families but X2 (2/13 → gone) with X1 7/13 → 4/13 and O4 12/13 → 11/13, F1 unchanged (46 firms, the filing's mid-week cash), the world in family (u 15.0/18.4/19.5/18.5 at week 13 against 15.3/18.4/19.6/18.7; pi 223.5 against 212.6). Not byte-identical by construction (the rows carry from week 2, JPN u 8.0 against 7.9); declared under §1.22 and named here. Next: A3.4 (the household sector's `depositsUSD` — the name shared with the banks' household line splits first).
387. **WIRES A3.4 — THE HOUSEHOLD SECTOR'S DEPOSITS ARE ITS ROWS AT THE REGION'S BANKS, CARRIED; A BANK'S HOUSEHOLD LINE IS ITS ROW; BYTE-IDENTICAL TO THE A3.3 TREE (2026-09-02).** The household sector joins the pools on the sector rows (`sectorRowAt`/`sectorCashOf`/`householdDepositsOf`/`householdDepositsAt`, `moveSectorRowsToBank` at a resolution and a merger): the pass mirror opens its rows FROM the store (they used to open from each bank's line), the projection lands the rows and writes each bank's `depositsUSD` AS its household row; `HouseholdState.depositsUSD` is deleted (the name it shared with the banks' line is now the line's alone) — the evolution takes the sector's deposits as an input from the caller (`microFeedback.householdDepositsUSD`, read off the ledger by 02) and carries no deposit stock; the seed's provisional sizing (`depositsToIncome`, macro/initialization.ts) rides a stash the three funding-split sites and close-seed read, and close-seed opens the household row at each bank at the line it strikes as the funding residual, so line and row cannot disagree at week 0. **What the first probe found (M5, one bank's sheet −0.23B at week 2):** the household loan pass and the evolution move a bank's household line DIRECTLY — a loan creates the borrower's deposit, amortization and interest destroy it, the bank's deposit interest credits it (§7.352's deposit events on the bank's own sheet) — and with the line now written from the row, those writes were lost while their equity legs stayed. Each bank's line delta from its own book is applied to its household row in 02b (`adjustSectorRow`) — one place, retired when those passes are account operations (A3.6). **Gates:** tsc, lint 349/354, hygiene (an `hs.depositsUSD` read fails), 124 tests; the two-week probe; `STATE_DUMP` differ IDENTICAL at week 4 and week 13 against the A3.3 tree's dumps, sorted logs identical, scoreboard 115 / 24, the money family clean. Next: A3.5, the treasury.
388. **HANDOFF (2026-09-02, end of the FINALIZATION session).** Eleven commits on `claude/master-plan-review-j2z20v` and `main`, tree clean: step 0's derivatives half (§7.382, one stage over one standing index, byte-identical, the week 9.0 → 5.5 s) and the two walks the line profile then named (§7.383, 5.5 → 4.4 s, byte-identical); A3.1 (§7.384), A3.2 (§7.385), A3.4 (§7.387) byte-identical to their bases; A3.3 (§7.386) the one declared re-baseline — **the run to judge against is §7.386's 115 / 24**, the money family clean throughout. **Two questions wait on the user, both recorded where they belong:** a bank's cash read (step 1b — reserves or zero) and the treasury's issuance rule (step 5). **What is next without them:** step 6 (A3.6, the bank lines: `cashReservesUSD` and the deposit classes once 02b's evolve, bank-lending's funding split, `absorbBankSheet` and the estates are account operations — A3.4's `adjustSectorRow` in 02b is the first of those operations and names the pattern), then 7 (A4). **Standing lessons this session added:** a spread over a seed object drops its `WeakMap` stashes (§7.384); an instrument that compares weeks keeps its own numbers, never a state object, because every week's state shares the persistent store (§7.385); the projection that writes a bank line FROM the rows must see every direct writer of that line as a row operation, or M5 opens (§7.387). Runs this session: one 13-week per commit (two on the merge's line-profile fixes shared one), the 2-week probe before every settlement slice, one 4-week dump per byte gate — §1.25 held.
389. **WIRES A3.6a — A BANK'S RESERVES ARE ITS ROW, CARRIED; THE LINE IS THE ROW; BYTE-IDENTICAL TO THE A3.4 TREE (2026-09-02).** The bank's own account at the central bank (`BANK:<ticker>`) joins the persistent store like every other party's: close-seed opens it at the reserves it strikes (`openAccount`, twice a defect), the mirror opens the pass row FROM it and prints a `[accounts]` line for a bank whose line moved without its row, the projection writes the pass's result INTO it and the sheet's `cashReservesUSD` from it (the line was `field + (balance − opening)`, which is `balance` exactly while the pass moves reserves by less than a factor of two — the differ confirmed it). Every direct writer of the line between two passes is a row operation, the pattern A3.4's `adjustSectorRow` set: 02b's rounding of the evolved sheet (`adjustBankReserves`), a merger (`moveBankReserves` beside `moveSectorRowsToBank`), the player's trade against a dealer desk (trade.ts), the harness's S6 reserve drain. A resolution needs none: the failed bank's cash leaves by wire (its BANK party settles on the row) and the shell's row is what the pay leaves, inside the 1e4 the shell check allows. The field's readers still read the field — the settled-cash reads in 07c/07f/repo/dealer-desks/funding-close, the resolution plan, bank-lending's buffers, the aggregate's scalar — and flip to `bankReservesOf` in 6b, when the field dies. **Gates:** tsc, lint 349/354, hygiene, 124 tests; the 2-week probe identical; `STATE_DUMP` differ IDENTICAL at week 4 against the A3.4 tree; the 13-week log identical in every printed number (115 / 24, the money family clean, no `[accounts]` line in 13 weeks). The 13-week dump was written at week 1 (I omitted `STATE_DUMP_WEEK`) and was not re-run — §1.25, one 13-week per commit; the week-4 differ and the week-13 log are the gate. Next: 6b.
390. **WIRES A3.6b — A BANK'S CORPORATE AND INSTITUTIONAL LINES ARE THE SUMS OF ITS DEPOSITORS' ROWS; §7.378's DELTA PROJECTION GONE; A DECLARED RE-BASELINE AT FLOAT-NOISE LEVEL, 116 / 24 (2026-09-02).** The projection wrote each bank's corporate and institutional line as `line + Σ(row − opening)` over the pass rows at it — §7.378's delta, which preserved whatever gap the line and its depositors had opened. Now the line IS Σ of the rows at the bank by class (`lineSumsByBank`, one walk, row order), and the mirror reports any line that moved without its rows since the last projection (`[accounts] … moved without its rows`), the measurement step 6 asked for: NONE over 13 weeks, and none at week 1 — the seed's lines (`Math.round(Σ max(0, opening cash))` by house bank) are already the exact sums of the rows it opens, so no seed change was needed. The corporate line follows a firm's house bank by construction (a merger and a resolution both `rekeyBankLinks`, so the depositors' rows move to the acquirer's index the pass after `absorbBankSheet` moved the line — the two views agree). **Gates:** tsc, lint 349/354, hygiene, 124 tests; the 2-week probe's only diff a foreign-ownership print at the 14th digit (Σ-order float noise, the shape §7.386 declared); `STATE_DUMP` differ at week 4 not identical for the same reason (102k diffs across 294 families, every one a cascade of that noise through the chaotic markets — the same count of firms, no line finding); the 13-week run 116 / 24, the money family clean, the money that is not anyone's 0.00B, no `[accounts]` line. Declared re-baseline: **the run to judge against is now 116 / 24** (§4 updated); the +1 is O4's dead-borrower loan appearing one more week, a trajectory move inside the same families. Next: 6c — the field readers flip to the rows (`bankReservesOf` first) and the five bank fields die.
391. **WIRES A3.6c-i — `cashReservesUSD` DELETED: A BANK'S RESERVES ARE ITS ACCOUNT, READ BY NAME EVERYWHERE; BYTE-IDENTICAL TO THE A3.6b TREE (2026-09-02).** The field is gone from `BankingSector` (the bank's and the region's aggregate alike). The one read is `bankReservesOf(v2, ticker)`; the 81 sites over 33 files fell into four kinds. **(1) Readers with a bank in hand** take the account: the settled-cash reads of 07c/07f/repo-clearing/dealer-desks/funding-close, the desk-capacity reads of every derivative market and fx-clearing and prime-brokerage (through `leverageHeadroomUSD(sheet, cashUSD)`), the audits, the traces, the news line, the UI, the harness (its identity check, its BANKCAP print, its S6 drain — now an account operation). **(2) Sheet-taking domain functions** take the cash as an argument, the A3.2 pattern (`institutionTotalAssetsUSD(e, cashUSD, …)`): `bankTotalAssetsUSD`, `leverageHeadroomUSD`, `sovereignBookCapacityUSD`, `liquidityDrivenSovereignFloorUSD`, `evolveBankingSector` (a `cashUSD` parameter beside the loan books — §5-WIRES D's shape; it no longer returns a reserves line), `planBankResolution`, `bankSheetAssetsUSD`, `restateBankSheetStatistics`, `repayCentralBankLoanUSD`, `applyBankFundingSplit`, `assumeBankBooks`, `fieldsOf`/`residualOf`, `sheetLinesUSD`; `dealersFromBanks` takes a reserves reader (the account in the week, the stash at the seed). **(3) The region's aggregate:** 02 reads Σ its banks' accounts into `microFeedback.bankReservesUSD` for the aggregate's evolve; 02b's asset weights read the accounts; the projection's aggregate delta line is gone; the UI's region statement sums the accounts. **(4) The seed** carries each bank's stated reserves in the opening-cash stash (`stashOpeningCash(sheet, …)` at the aggregate's sizing, the bank's split of it, the three funding sites' `+= corp/sme/inst`, close-seed's over-funded branch) until close-seed opens the account at it — the same channel a firm's opening cash rides, and the seed's dealer headroom reads the stash (before close-seed) exactly as it read the field. **One artifact kept for identity:** the evolution rounded the reserves line to the dollar every week; 02b now rounds the account by the same fraction (`adjustBankReserves(…, Math.round(r) − r)`) — a stated artifact, deletable in a declared re-baseline. `mergeBankSheets` no longer moves cash (the merger's `moveBankReserves` does); the harness dump carries `reserves` off the ledger beside `cash`. **Gates:** tsc, lint 349/354, hygiene, 124 tests (the resolution test replays the cash legs on two local accounts); the 2-week probe identical; `STATE_DUMP` differ at week 4 AND week 13 identical but for the deleted field (16 banks × `cashReservesUSD` MISSING-IN-B); the 13-week log identical, 116 / 24. Next: 6c-ii, the four deposit lines.
392. **WIRES A3.6c-ii — `corporateDepositsUSD` AND `institutionalDepositsUSD` DELETED: A BANK'S DEPOSIT LINES ARE ONE LEDGER READ, PASSED BESIDE THE SHEET; A DECLARED RE-BASELINE AT FLOAT-NOISE LEVEL, 116 / 23 (2026-09-02).** `DepositLines` (domain/banking.ts: household, corporate, institutional, SME) is the read of a bank's four lines off the ledger — the household sector's row and the pools' rows at the bank (A3.3/A3.4's reads) and the firms' and institutions' accounts whose house bank it is, summed in the holders' order, the order the pass rows summed in (`corporateDepositsAt`/`institutionalDepositsAt`/`depositLinesAt`; `bankDepositLines(ctx, ticker)` in a stage, `stateDepositLines(state, ticker)` in an audit, the UI or the harness). Every sheet-taking function that read a line takes it beside the cash: `stressedOutflowUSD`, `liquidityDrivenSovereignFloorUSD`, `evolveBankingSector` (a `deposits` parameter; the corporate deposit interest and M2 read it; the return no longer carries the two lines), `repayCentralBankLoanUSD`, `depositsOf`, `bankAssumedLiabilitiesUSD`, `planBankResolution`, `restateBankSheetStatistics`, `sheetLinesUSD`, `fieldsOf`/`residualOf`; the region's evolve takes `microFeedback.bankDepositLines` (Σ its banks', from 02). **Gone with the fields:** the projection's line writes and A3.6b's mirror gate; 02b's per-bank reconciliation of the two lines against the holders' cash (§7.288's meter — a tautology once the line IS the holders' cash; `cashReconcileByClassUSD` keeps only the SME meter) and its two by-bank maps; M3's line-versus-holders checks (the "balances with no bank" check stays); `absorbBankSheet`'s two line moves (the depositors re-key to the acquirer, `rekeyBankLinks`, and the lines follow). **The seed** opens every firm's and institution's account BEFORE close-seed (it read the lines off the sheet; it reads them off the accounts now) and sizes the lines to the dollar as the field did — the 2-week probe found a sub-dollar change in the funding residual moved EUR's week-2 unemployment print by 0.1pt (the seed's household net worth is struck on that residual), so the dollar rounding is kept and named at the site. **Gates:** tsc, lint 348/354 (one warning fewer), hygiene, 124 tests (the resolution test states the lines beside the sheet); the 2-week probe identical but for the deleted reconcile print and 14th-digit noise in one foreign-ownership print (the corporate interest's Σ order — §7.386's shape); the week-4 differ a cascade of that noise (105k diffs across 294 families, the same count of firms); the 13-week run 116 / 23, the money family clean, the money that is not anyone's 0.00B — O3 did not fire on this trajectory and stays a §6 row. Declared re-baseline: **the run to judge against is now 116 / 23**. Next: 6c-iii, the household and SME fields.
393. **WIRES A3.6c-iii — `depositsUSD` AND `smeDepositsUSD` DELETED: A BANK SHEET CARRIES NO BALANCE; THE EVOLUTION AND THE HOUSEHOLD PASS REPORT THEIR LINE MOVES AND 02b POSTS THEM; BYTE-IDENTICAL TO THE A3.6c-ii TREE (2026-09-02). STEP 6 CLOSED.** The last two fields go. `evolveBankingSector` takes the household line from `deposits` and returns `{ sheet, householdLineUSD }` — the line its two flows leave (the loan interest debited, the deposit interest credited), TO THE DOLLAR, the stated rounding the field carried; the household pass (`runBankHouseholdLending`) writes no line and its four flows (mortgage origination and discharge, consumer-credit origination, amortization) are what 02b assembles, in the pass's own order, into the one `adjustSectorRow` on the household sector's row at the bank — A3.4's delta line, now built from the flows rather than read off a field. The buffers read the row (`householdDepositsAt`: 07c/07f/repo-clearing/dealer-desks, `raiseCentralBankLoanUSD`), the news line and the UI read it, the audits' M3 line-versus-row checks are gone (tautologies), `absorbBankSheet` moves no deposit line at all (the sector rows move by `moveSectorRowsToBank`, the accounts follow their house bank), the projection writes nothing onto a bank sheet but its own reserves row. **The seed:** the provisional household line the migrations split by rides a second stash (`stashSeedHouseholdLine`/`seedHouseholdLineOf`) — the aggregate's sizing, the bank's share, the funding-side rewrite, `applyBankFundingSplit` — until close-seed strikes the residual and opens the household row at it; no aggregate literal carries a deposit sum. **Gone with the last meter:** 02b's SME reconcile assertion and the whole `cashReconcileUSD`/`cashReconcileByClassUSD` apparatus (context, state, the harness print) — the deposit lines ARE the holders' accounts, so "money moved with no instruction behind it" has nothing left to measure; the overdraft count it also kept stays. **Gates:** tsc, lint 347/354, hygiene, 124 tests; the 2-week probe identical to the A3.6c-ii tree's; `STATE_DUMP` differ IDENTICAL at week 4 and week 13 but for the two deleted fields and the dump's new `lines` column (every bank's four lines off the ledger, beside `reserves` and `cash`); the 13-week log identical, 116 / 23, the money family clean. **Step 6 is closed:** a `BankingSector` carries no balance; a bank's money is its account and its depositors' accounts, read by name. Next: step 7, A4.
394. **WIRES A4 — SETTLEMENT'S NINE-WAY SWITCH DELETED; ITS TALLIES ARE READS OF THE ROWS' DELTAS; THE HYGIENE MONEY-FIELD BUDGET RETIRED; BYTE-IDENTICAL TO THE A3.6c-iii TREE (2026-09-02). PART I CLOSED BUT FOR THE TWO QUESTIONS.** The pass netted every party on its id, then resolved each net through a switch on the party's kind — company (bank or depositor), institution, pool (split across the region's banks by share), household (the same), bank, its securities account, the clearing house, its credit account, the treasury, the central bank — to find which bank line and which tally it moved, and a third step netted the interbank residual. The store already applies every row by the one rule and knows every row's bank, class and opening balance, so the switch and the interbank step are gone: `settledTallies` (ledger/accounts.ts) is one walk over the rows — a bank's reserve movement is its own row's delta, deposits its credit wrote are its CREATED row, its securities account's money the SECURITIES row, the treasury's account the TREASURY rows, the central bank's issuance and the clearing house's residual the VOID rows, money on a holder with no bank the depositor-class rows at nowhere; a bank's own income and expense — the one thing a row's delta cannot tell from a customer's money — are its two own-account parties (its BANK party and its goods-market self, both on the reserve row) netted as they settled (`ownNetByParty`, kept in `side`), the same two sums the switch kept and added in the same order, so the equity leg is the same float. The report loses the five tallies nothing read (the per-class deposit deltas, the T+1 household deferral A2 retired) and `centralBankResidualUSD` loses the deferral term; the equity apply iterates the equity map. `regionOfParty` (a lookup for the official-settlement leg, not a write) and the per-reason ledgers stay. A row a party has no account for is still counted (`accountRowsUnmapped`, M7) and, as the store always did, not applied on either side — the switch applied the known side and counted the other as unresolved; no run has produced one. **Hygiene:** the money-field write budget and its spread-form twin (`cashUSD`, `cashReservesUSD`, the four deposit lines — ratcheted 23 → 16 → 2 over §7.241–290) are retired: the fields do not exist, the type refuses them; the cast-hidden READ rule stays. **Gates:** tsc, lint 347/354, hygiene, 124 tests; the 2-week probe identical; `STATE_DUMP` differ IDENTICAL at week 4 and week 13; the 13-week log identical, 116 / 23, the money family clean. **Part I of §5-FINALIZATION is closed** but for step 1b (a bank's cash read) and step 5 (A3.5, the treasury's issuance rule), both the user's. Next: Part II, step 8 — the estates close consignments and contracts (O5, 13/13 weeks).
395. **WIRES A3.1b — A BANK'S CASH IS ITS RESERVES; A DECLARED ECONOMICS RE-BASELINE, 121 / 27 (2026-09-02).** The user answered the open question by the rules ("the response is in the rules, just apply them"): rule 3 (one representation of one real thing) and §7.379's conclusion. `cashOf` of a bank with a sheet reads `bankReservesOf`; the seed opens no company account for a bank (its money is the reserve row close-seed opens); the resolution shell's `resetAccount` is gone (the shell has no account until a pass opens one at zero at its acquirer — its estate). The corporate rules that read a firm's cash — stage 08's surplus-cash prepay and deleveraging, 05's rationing threshold, the merger acquirer gate, labour's cash-sign test — now see a bank's reserves, unguarded: rule 2 forbids a guard that clamps the symptom, rule 8 says a bank retiring its own paper from surplus reserves IS its treasury. **Gates:** tsc, lint 347/354, hygiene, 124 tests; the 2-week probe shows the economics moving at once (USA household income 752.6 → 753.7B, the price index 138.62 → 138.85 at week 2); the one 13-week run 121 / 27 — the same audit families as §7.392 plus O3 (a merger's residue, back on this trajectory), the money family clean, the money that is not anyone's 0.00B. Declared re-baseline: **the run to judge against is now 121 / 27** (§4 updated); measured once, at the end (rule 12). Next: A3.5, by rule N.
396. **WIRES A3.5 — THE TREASURY IS ONE ROW; THE UNFUNDED-DEFICIT SIDE MAP IS GONE BY RULE N; 121 / 27 (2026-09-02). PART I CLOSED.** The user answered the issuance question by the rules: §5-WIRES N ("no pending state outside the journal — the unfunded deficit is a dated wire, not a side map") and rule 3. **The account:** `treasuryAccountUSD` and `waysAndMeansUSD` are deleted from `CentralBank`; the government party's persistent row carries both as its two signs (`treasuryNetOf`, `treasuryAccountOf` = max(0, net), `waysAndMeansOf` = max(0, −net)); the seed sizes the operating balance into a stash and opens the row before close-seed; the mirror opens the pass row from it and the projection writes it back; `centralBankAssetsUSD(cb, advance)` and the identity residual take the advance as an argument; every reader (the fiscal stage's cash bridge, the ways-and-means interest, the audits M1/M6/F2, the snapshot, the trace, the UI, the harness) reads by name; M4's "overdrawn treasury" and the harness's negative-TGA check are gone (the negative side IS the advance, granted by rule) — the harness now counts region-weeks with the advance drawn. `compareToBooks`, the first slice's gate (§7.377), is retired with the last field it compared; `accountMismatchUSD/Worst` leave the report, the state and M7. **The issuance rule:** `pendingUnfundedDeficitUSD` is deleted (the region, 07c's and 07f's withdrawal roll-forward, the fiscal stage's accumulator). Between blocks the treasury spends its balance and then the advance; at the block (week 13n) it issues `waysAndMeansOf + 13 × marketFundedDeficitUSD` — the advance repaid, the coming quarter's bond share of the deficit at the current run rate; withdrawn auction paper rolls into nothing (the account runs lower and the next block sees the advance). **Gates:** tsc, lint 347/354, hygiene, 124 tests; the 2-week probe identical; the 13-week log identical to §7.395's on every weekly print through week 13's summary line — the block's new size shows only in week-13 detail lines (O4 30 → 31 borrowers, P1 1075 → 1069 issuers); 121 / 27, the same families and counts, the money family clean, the money that is not anyone's 0.00B; the TGA now ranges 0.0..66.6B with the advance drawn in 4 region-weeks (it never was before: the buffer is the treasury's to spend). No 4-week dump: 1b's tree had none to differ against and the log identity through week 12 is the gate (§1.25, one run). **Part I of §5-FINALIZATION is closed.** Next: Part II, step 8.
397. **FINALIZATION STEP 8 — THE ESTATES CLOSE CONSIGNMENTS AND CONTRACTS; O5's SHIPMENTS 13/13 → 2/13, ITS CONTRACTS 6/13 → 1/13; 105 / 27 (2026-09-02).** The survey first (rule 1.25: read before running): O5's "contracts with a dead party" row is the DERIVATIVES book, not the supply-contract table — the lifecycle already closed out a defaulted party at its class's next settle, so a firm dying in 08 after its class had settled stood on its contracts until the next week; and O5's consignments were, in weeks 1–2, the seed's pipeline naming `<region>_SEED_SUPPLIER` (no party) and from week 3 the consignments still at sea to firms that died this week or last (1,885 and 834 of them at week 6, to six dead firms), which goods-arrival already scrapped on landing. **Built:** (1) `closeOutDerivativesOfParty` (derivative-lifecycle.ts) — the settle's DEFAULTED branch for every class at once, called at the estate's opening (the firm's COMPANY party; a resolved bank's BANK party too), paid through the estate's account like any claim; (2) goods-arrival delivers a consignment to a dead buyer with an OPEN estate (a machine or an operating purchase landing at a receivership is scrapped on landing — nothing to commission, no week to use it); (3) the workout's inventory is the real rows — finished stock AND input lots (`totalInputValueUSD`), re-read each week — and the week's slice sells input lots to the same peers by wire (`moveInputUnits`: FIFO off the estate's chains, one lot on the buyer at the cost it left at) and scraps the unsold rest (`scrapInputUnits`); a closing estate scraps its last lots and what is still on its way (`scrapConsignmentsTo`, the carrier's write-off); (4) a dead firm nobody has a claim on opens no estate and has its consignments scrapped at death, not on landing; (5) the seed's pipeline names the origin's segment pool of the good's industry as its seller. The audit reads a consignment to an open estate as live. **Gates:** tsc, lint 347/354, hygiene, 124 tests; the 2-week probe identical but for O5's two seed-pipeline lines, gone; the one 13-week run 105 / 27 (from 121), the money family clean, the goods audit silent: O5 shipments 2/13 (worst 11 consignments, last 4) and O5 contracts 1/13 (12 contracts, 0.09B) — both the residues of a non-bank merger (an acquired firm is not active, opens no estate, and the merger re-keys only a bank's contracts), step 9's row. Declared re-baseline (the estates recover more): **the run to judge against is now 105 / 27**. Next: step 9.
398. **FINALIZATION STEP 9 — NOTHING STAYS BEHIND ON A MERGED FIRM; O3, N2 AND O5 CLEAN; 98 / 22 (2026-09-02).** Read first: N2's one tranche was `consolidateTranches` keeping a target tranche's id when it consolidated alone (a bucket of one is pushed as it is); O3's 71 rows (0.01B) were the target's COMMERCIAL PAPER on the money funds' books — the one company-keyed kind the exchange did not swap; O5's two merger weeks were a non-bank target's derivatives (only a bank's re-key through `rekeyBankLinks`) and the consignments still on their way to it (an acquired firm is not active, opens no estate, and goods-arrival scrapped them on landing). **Built, in the merger:** every mergeable target tranche is renamed `<acquirer>-ACQ<week>-<old id>` before consolidation (the lineage inside); commercial paper joins the equity/bond/loan exchange (the target's paper back to its clearing house, the acquirer's out to the holder, value unchanged); a non-bank target's standing derivatives novate to the acquirer (`COMPANY:target` → `COMPANY:acquirer` on the book); consignments in transit to the target are the acquirer's to take delivery of; the target's finished stock and input lots move onto the acquirer's books by wire (`moveOutputUnits`, `moveInputUnits`); and its supply contracts name the acquirer on either side (`novateContracts`, engine2/contracts.ts — by either key the table stores, ticker or id). **Gates:** tsc, lint 347/354, hygiene, 124 tests; no probe (the merger runs at week 13 only); the one 13-week run 98 / 22 (from 105 / 27): O3 gone, N2 gone (the names family clean), O5's two rows gone, the money family clean; every weekly print identical to §7.397's through week 13 (only the two merger-week violation counts move). **The run to judge against is now 98 / 22.** What Part II still owes: step 10 (O4, the facility tranche and the loan row as one ledger, 12/13) and step 11 (a firm with no house bank).

399. **FINALIZATION STEP 10 — THE FACILITY TRANCHE IS THE LENDER'S LOAN: ONE LEDGER; O4 CLEAN; 81 / 19 (2026-09-02).** Read first: the bank's `businessLoans` carried one COMPANY_FACILITY row per facility tranche, written at settlement from a credit-event queue (`creditEventsThisWeek`, pushed by stage 08's `recordCredit`, 02b, 07f, pe-lifecycle and the overdraft sweep) and re-synced to the ladders in 02b every week — two representations of one loan, and O4's 12/13 was the drift between the syncs (a maintenance bridge's row carried the week's slice, a merged bank's rows kept naming the absorbed lender and were then written off the acquirer as "left the ladders"). **Built:** the tranche row carries its issuer (`issuerRef`, engine2/tranches.ts; a freed row carries nothing a scan could mistake for a live tranche), and a lender's book is one scan of the store — `facilityRowsOf(v2, bank)`, `facilityBookOf(v2, bank)`, `facilitiesOfBorrower(v2, firm)` (the same rows from the borrower's side). `BankLoan.borrowerKind` keeps only the SME pool; every reader of a bank's credit book takes the facility book beside the sheet (`businessLoanBookOf`, `loanBooksOf`, `regionLoanBooksUSD`, `bankRwaUSD`, `bankTotalAssetsUSD`, `leverageHeadroomUSD`, `sovereignBookCapacityUSD`, the resolution plan and its chooser, `restateBankSheetStatistics`, `fieldsOf`/`residualOf`, the funding split and both seed migrations, the desks, the CDS/IRS/FX/commodity capacity reads, the PD, the profile, the audits, the UI); the region's evolve takes `microFeedback.bankLoanBooks` (Σ its banks', from 02). **Gone:** the credit-event channel end to end (context, stage-deps, stage 08's shard accumulators and marks, the back worker's shipped credits, `makeRecordCredit`, settlement's 4b), bank-lending's facility sync, its `facilityNetOriginationUSD` write-off and defect, 02b's per-bank facility scan. The estate's bank claim is the facility rows on the dead firm's ladder (one claim per lender), and its write-down retires the ladder face and moves the lender's equity with no loan row to edit; a bank merger wires the target's facilities to the acquirer (`moveFacilityLender`, the hole §7.339's comment named); O4 now reads the rows themselves — every facility names a live borrower (active or in an open estate) and a lender with a sheet. The seed opens with no facilities on any ladder, so its funding split, sovereign headroom and NIM revenue are unchanged by construction. **Gates:** tsc, lint 347/354, hygiene, 124 tests (the resolution and pricing tests state the facility book beside the sheet); the 2-week probe identical on every headline through week 2 but for O4's rows, gone, and a 4th-significant-digit move in week 2's cleared spreads — Σ-order float noise (the book now sums SME rows then facilities in ladder-row order; margins and principals agree by construction, §7.386's shape); the one 13-week run 81 / 19 (from 98 / 22): both O4 rows gone, the money family clean, the money that is not anyone's 0.00B, the same standing families (P1/P3/F1/W2/X1) plus X2's futures-carry row back on this trajectory (2/13). Declared re-baseline: **the run to judge against is now 81 / 19** (§4 updated). Next: step 11, a firm with no house bank.

400. **FINALIZATION STEP 11 — A FIRM WITH NO HOUSE BANK; PART II CLOSED; BYTE-IDENTICAL TO §7.399 (2026-09-02).** Read first: the only firms without a house bank are the banks (the seed picks one for every other firm and every fund by `mandateAllocator`, a newborn is picked one before its birth facility is funded, a resolution and a merger re-key the link through `rekeyBankLinks`), and the class had two live paths left — stage 08's revolver on a liquidity shortfall and its revolver on a withdrawn refinancing, both `facilityBankTicker: homeBankTicker`, which for a bank is a facility with no lender (the ledger's `names no lending bank` defect, the way §7.372's run died at week 12 on a bridge). **Built:** neither revolver is drawn by a firm with no house bank — a bank's shortfall is its own book (its reserves, the central bank's window) and a bank's maturity is repaid from it, as the bridge already was (`hasHouseBank`, §7.372); the merger's bank block keeps ONE re-key (`rekeyBankLinks` moves the facility rows and the customers' links; step 10's separate lender loop is gone) and re-reads the acquirer's statistics after it; M3 counts a house bank with no sheet as no bank (`… sit with no live house bank`), the measurement that the re-keys happened. **Gates:** tsc, lint 347/354, hygiene, 124 tests; the one 13-week run byte-identical to §7.399's on every log line and the week-13 dump (`DIFF_STATE` IDENTICAL — no bank drew a revolver on this trajectory, so the paths were closed before they fired); 81 / 19, the money family clean, the money that is not anyone's 0.00B. **Part II of §5-FINALIZATION is closed.** Next: Part III, step 12 — the registry of every stated number.

401. **FINALIZATION STEP 12 — THE REGISTRY OF STATED NUMBERS OPENS; PART III LANDS AS A RATCHET; BYTE-IDENTICAL TO §7.400 (2026-09-02).** Measured first: the engine (`src/engine`, `src/engine2`, `src/domain`) carries 1,387 fractional literals outside comments and `toFixed` — every one a shape claim with nobody's name on it, most of them the industry registry's tables and the seed's ratios. A lint rule that errors on them is a rule nobody could pass; the repo's form for that is the hygiene RATCHET (§4's budgets: may fall, never rise), so R lands as one. **Built:** `src/domain/stated.ts` — `stated({ id, value, kind, owner, reason, replacedBy })` returns the value so the declaration IS the constant (a second declaration of an id must agree or it is a defect), `statedRegistry()`/`statedCounts()` read it, and the five kinds are rule 19's (TECHNOLOGY, PREFERENCE, POLICY as primitives; SHAPE and RESOLUTION as debts with a scheduled death). Eight declarations open it: the seed's business and consumer loan-book ratios (`seedLoanBookUSD`, 0.040 and 0.070 of GDP — replaced by a burn-in in which the banks lend), the seed's firm-size decay (0.80 — the curve behind every bank's market share, so the household split by share, and the insurers' and managers' slices), the insurers' 0.42 of the institutional pool (replaced by premiums written, §5-INS), and the four endpoints of the two PREFERENCE ranges (§7.344's brains: patience on [4, 52] weeks, risk aversion on [0.5, 2]). The scoreboard prints `R · stated numbers registered: 8 (preference 4, shape 4)` every run. **The ratchet** (scripts/check-hygiene.sh): fractional literals in the engine outside the registry, 1,381 measured with the eight moved, budget 1,381 — the way to add a fraction is to declare it. **Gates:** tsc, lint 347/354, hygiene (the new budget included), 125 tests (one pure test on the registry: the declaration is the constant, a disagreeing redeclaration throws); the one 13-week run byte-identical to §7.400's on every line but the new scoreboard line, the week-13 dump IDENTICAL (the values did not move, only their owners). **Part III lands** as the registry plus the ratchet; the ~1,380 literals still outside it are the ratchet's work, one area per commit as each number gets its owner and its death. Next: Part IV, step 13 — per-instrument price clearing for the corporate books.

402. **FINALIZATION STEP 13, FIRST SLICE — EVERY HOLDER'S FILL IS A WIRE; W2 16 → 8–10 REGION-KINDS; BYTE-IDENTICAL TO §7.401 (2026-09-02).** Read first, then traced (a `W2_TRACE=1` line in audit/wires.ts prints every net): the house's equity net was −66.7B in USA at week 2 — not a missing holder but a WRONG PRICE: the register's fill wires were valued at the change in a row's VALUE, which carries the revaluation of the shares the holder kept (a mark, which is not a move); the credit books' small nets were the lead's underwriting residual written straight into its desk inventory; the sovereign nets were the bill buyers booking cost against a treasury leg delivered at face; and the corporate primaries' paper legs are ALREADY the tranche ledger's wires (issuer → house at issue, W3), so adding them at the CCP double-counted (the first 13-week run showed it: CORP_BOND +1.4B and W3 lit). **Built:** (1) the non-register holders wire their fills through `clearedBookDelta` — the banks' bond and bill buckets (`BANK_SECURITIES`, before against after per bucket instrument), the central bank's fills (`wireCentralBankFills`, central-bank-demand.ts), the corporate treasuries' bill rows (`COMPANY`), the households' direct-equity sells (`HOUSEHOLD`, at the print); (2) the holdings store's write-back prices every share wire at the week's ONE mark per instrument — what any appended row of it carries — so the seller's wire and the buyer's agree (`markById`); the equity primary's paper leg is at the rounded print every row carries; (3) the paying agent wires the ISSUER's side of an equity action (a buyback: house → issuer; a placement: issuer → house — equity has no ladder to carry it), and the merger's equity exchange names both issuers (target shares cancelled, acquirer shares issued); (4) the lead's residual is a wire (house → lead's desk for the credit books; issuer → lead's desk for equity); (5) the treasury's bill leg is valued at what the register buyers booked (face less their rebates; the desks book face). The new reasons are registered in payment-category.ts. **Gates:** tsc, lint 347/354, hygiene (the two literal kind comparisons the slice first typed were replaced by `heldInShares`), 125 tests; four 2-week probes with the trace (the diagnosis), and — recorded against §1.25 — TWO 13-week runs on this commit, the first the one that exposed the double count; the final run 81 / 19 with every weekly print and the week-13 dump IDENTICAL to §7.401's (a wire is a record; nothing moved), W2 now 8–10 region-kinds a week instead of 16: the equity and sovereign kinds are clean, what remains is CORP_BOND and LEVERAGED_LOAN at 0.0–0.45B — the issuer-keyed register applies a redemption pro rata across ALL of an issuer's paper while the ladder retires ONE tranche, so the two sides cannot meet at the house until the register is keyed by tranche in face (step 13's second slice, P proper). Next: that slice.

403. **STEP 13's REMAINDER, DESIGNED (2026-09-02; no engine change).** Read before building: the credit register keys a row by the ISSUER's company id (07b/07d/07f append `instrumentId: company.id`; the seed writes the same), the auctions clear one instrument per issuer per book, corporate actions scale a holder's rows by the issuer's float ratio (`settleCorporateActionOnHolders(issuerId, kind, preFace, postFace)` from stage08-back's ladder walk), accruals are keyed `kind:issuerId`, estates index rows by issuer, the merger exchanges rows by issuer, the desks carry positions by issuer, O3 asks whether the instrument id names a live company — while the ladder side (W3) wires every tranche by its own id. The two cannot meet at the house until the register is keyed by tranche; that is sub-slice 13b, listed with its ~25 sites under §5-FINALIZATION step 13, and 13c–13e follow it. Two facts the design rests on: the tranche store already carries `issuerRef` (step 10), so a tranche id resolves to its issuer in one read; and a per-issuer fill splits across the issuer's tranches pro rata to face with no new stated number — it is what the pro-rata corporate action already assumed. Runs this design cost: none.

404. **FINALIZATION STEP 13, SECOND SLICE — THE DESKS ARE HOLDERS OF RECORD; W2 13/13 → 4/13 AT DUST; O6 OPENS SILENT; A DECLARED RE-BASELINE, 81 / 20 (2026-09-02).** §7.403's diagnosis was wrong about the cause and right about the design: the credit books' W2 residue (−0.27B USA CORP_BOND at week 1, every week) was not the issuer-keyed register — a region|kind net cannot see the key — but two holders-of-record defects, found by a one-week script against the ledger itself after a harness trace had first suggested minted paper (it had double-counted the desks: the ownership battery already folds the region's desk view into `held`; the register plus the desks is exactly the ladders' face, week after week — O6 below now says so every run). (1) A corporate action scaled the REGISTER's rows by the issuer's float ratio and left the DESKS' positions standing until the next auction's `reconcileHolderPrincipal` paid them down a week later, with no wire; the house was short by the desks' share of each week's retirements. The paying agent now scales the named desks' `corporate bond` / `leveraged loan` positions by the same ratio in the same pass (shared-helpers.ts, on the live sheet — the channel is consumed by then, §7.250), the issuer redeeming (or being paid) with the same `holder of record` legs, the paper wired desk ↔ house; the paydown stays as the guard, its desk leg now a wire too, and 07f's CP maturity on a desk is a wire. (2) Under firm commitment the pre-action face counted the book's TAKE while the tranche created was the whole DEAL, so the pro-rata action scaled every register holder up by the lead's residual as a placement paid to the issuer (`primaryPlacedUSD`, one expression for the pre-action face and the tranche). **Also:** `O6 corporate paper held = issued` (audit/ownership.ts: the register's rows plus every named desk against the ladders, per region and kind — the corporate twin of O1; silent 13/13), the one audit tolerance declared in the registry (`AUDIT_BOOKS_TOLERANCE`, a RESOLUTION number; O1, O2, O5 and O6 read it; the fractional-literal ratchet falls 1381 → 1377), a `W2_TRACE=1` net line and an `OWN_TRACE=1` coverage line in the harness. **Gates:** tsc, lint 347/354, hygiene, 125 tests; five 2-week probes and two 1-week scripts (the diagnosis, §1.25's "read first" honoured in the end by reading the ledger, not the print); the one 13-week run 81 / 20: the money family clean, O6 silent, W2 4/13 weeks on EQUITY only at 0.00–0.01B (rounding between the print every row carries and the desks' unit price — named, not chased, rule 10), X2 off this trajectory; the economics move at week 2 (the desks are paid at the retirement, not a week later: pi 138.85 → 139.15) — a declared re-baseline: **the run to judge against is now 81 / 20** (§4 updated). Next: 13b, the register keyed by tranche — P's foundation.
